import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# Ensure the project root is in sys.path
base_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
base_dir = str(base_path)
if base_dir not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, base_dir)

# Safely load .env from project root if present
env_file = base_path / '.env'
if env_file.is_file():
    try:
        with open(env_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            for line in f:
                line = line.strip()
                if line and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:
                    k, v = line.split('=', 1)
                    k = k.strip()
                    v = v.strip().strip("'\"")
                    if k:
                        os.environ[k] = v
    except Exception:
        pass

from app import create_app

app = create_app(os.environ.get('FLASK_CONFIG', 'default'))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    port = int(os.environ.get('PORT', 5000))
    app.run(host='127.0.0.1', port=port, debug=True)

FILE PATH: config.py

import os
from pathlib import Path

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

def load_dotenv_if_present(env_path: Path):
    """Safely loads key-value pairs from .env file into os.environ without requiring external packages."""
    if not env_path.is_file():
        return
    try:
        with open(env_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            for line in f:
                line = line.strip()
                if not line or line.startswith('#'):
                    continue
                if '=' in line:
                    key, val = line.split('=', 1)
                    key = key.strip()
                    val = val.strip().strip("'\"")
                    if key:
                        os.environ[key] = val
    except Exception:
        pass

# Automatically load .env from project directory
load_dotenv_if_present(BASE_DIR / '.env')

class Config:
    # All secrets, keys, and provider settings are strictly imported from the .env file
    SECRET_KEY = os.environ.get('SECRET_KEY')
    UPLOAD_FOLDER = os.environ.get('UPLOAD_FOLDER', str(BASE_DIR / 'instance' / 'uploads'))
    MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH = int(os.environ.get('MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH', 10 * 1024 * 1024))
    ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = {'json'}
    DEBUG = False
    TESTING = False

    # AI Provider & Model Configuration (Imported strictly from .env)
    AI_ENABLED = os.environ.get('AI_ENABLED', '').lower() in ('true', '1', 'yes')
    AI_PROVIDER = os.environ.get('AI_PROVIDER', '').strip().lower()

    GEMINI_API_KEY = os.environ.get('GEMINI_API_KEY', '').strip()
    GEMINI_MODEL = os.environ.get('GEMINI_MODEL', '').strip()

    OLLAMA_BASE_URL = os.environ.get('OLLAMA_BASE_URL', '').strip()
    OLLAMA_MODEL = os.environ.get('OLLAMA_MODEL', '').strip()

    AI_REQUEST_TIMEOUT = int(os.environ.get('AI_REQUEST_TIMEOUT', 60))
    AI_MAX_RETRIES = int(os.environ.get('AI_MAX_RETRIES', 2))
    AI_CACHE_ENABLED = os.environ.get('AI_CACHE_ENABLED', 'true').lower() in ('true', '1', 'yes')
    AI_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = int(os.environ.get('AI_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS', 3600))
    AI_MAX_CONTEXT_SIZE = int(os.environ.get('AI_MAX_CONTEXT_SIZE', 32000))

class DevelopmentConfig(Config):
    DEBUG = True

class TestingConfig(Config):
    TESTING = True
    UPLOAD_FOLDER = str(BASE_DIR / 'instance' / 'test_uploads')

class ProductionConfig(Config):
    DEBUG = False

config_by_name = {
    'development': DevelopmentConfig,
    'testing': TestingConfig,
    'production': ProductionConfig,
    'default': DevelopmentConfig
}



================================================================================
FILE PATH: pack_project.py
================================================================================
import os

OUTPUT_FILE = "project_content.txt"
EXCLUDED_DIRS = {".git", "__pycache__", "venv", "reports","source_code","database", "app-build","old_server_data","data",".venv"}
EXCLUDED_EXTENSIONS = {".pyc", ".ico", ".png", ".jpg", ".exe", ".dll", ".lib", ".obj",".db",".zip",".mp4",".log"}

def pack_project():
    root_dir = os.getcwd()
    
    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as outfile:
        outfile.write("===========================================\n")
        outfile.write(f"PROJECT DOCUMENTATION DUMP\n")
        outfile.write(f"Root: {root_dir}\n")
        outfile.write("===========================================\n\n")

        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(root_dir):
            # Modify dirs in-place to skip excluded directories
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in EXCLUDED_DIRS]
            
            for file in files:
                if file == OUTPUT_FILE:
                    continue
                    
                _, ext = os.path.splitext(file)
                if ext.lower() in EXCLUDED_EXTENSIONS:
                    continue
                
                # Check for "audit" or huge logs that we might have missed in dirs
                if "log" in file.lower() and ext == ".txt":
                    continue

                path = os.path.join(root, file)
                rel_path = os.path.relpath(path, root_dir)
                
                print(f"Adding: {rel_path}")
                
                outfile.write("="*80 + "\n")
                outfile.write(f"FILE PATH: {rel_path}\n")
                outfile.write("="*80 + "\n")
                
                try:
                    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as infile:
                        content = infile.read()
                        outfile.write(content)
                except Exception as e:
                    outfile.write(f"[ERROR READING FILE: {e}]")
                
                outfile.write("\n\n")

    print(f"\nSuccess! Project content saved to {OUTPUT_FILE}")
    input("Press Enter to exit...")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    pack_project()

FILE PATH: README.md

# Azure ARM Security Analyzer & Visualizer

A standalone Python Flask web application that allows users to upload Azure Resource Manager (ARM) JSON templates, interactively visualize the provisioned Azure cloud infrastructure topology, and perform deterministic static security analysis to identify misconfigurations before deployment.

---

## 1. Project Overview

- **Location**: `E:\AzureProject\v1\azure-security-analyzer`
- **Application Type**: Independent Python Flask Web Application + Cytoscape.js & Native SVG Frontend
- **Target File Formats**: Azure Resource Manager (ARM) JSON Templates (`.json`)
- **Key Concepts Synthesized**:
  - **Topology Mapping**: Inspired by [AzureResourceVisualizer](https://github.com/ytechie/AzureResourceVisualizer) (dependency graphs, network associations, category mapping, Azure-style node representations).
  - **Security Auditing**: Inspired by Microsoft's [Template Analyzer](https://github.com/Azure/template-analyzer) (deterministic rule scanning, severity ratings, prescriptive remediation, comprehensive coverage).

> **Note on Independence**: This application is completely standalone. It does not import, invoke, or depend on any binaries, scripts, packages, or build artifacts from either reference repository.

---

## 2. Key Features

- **ARM Template Ingestion & Validation**:
  - Strict JSON syntax and Azure ARM schema validation.
  - Safe file handling with collision-free UUID4 identifiers, directory traversal guards, and file size limits (10 MB).
  - Distinguishes between syntax errors, non-ARM JSON structures, and valid templates with analysis warnings.
- **Safe ARM Expression Resolver (No Python `eval()`)**:
  - Safely evaluates `parameters('paramName')` (using default values).
  - Evaluates `variables('varName')` with chained dependency resolution.
  - Recursively resolves `concat(...)`, `format('{0}-{1}', ...)`, `if(...)`, `equals(...)`, and `resourceId(...)`.
  - Gracefully retains runtime/complex functions (e.g. `reference()`, `listKeys()`) as symbolic placeholders without crashing or producing false positives.
- **Dynamic Graph Layout Selector**:
  - **Tiered Architecture (Default)**: Organizes cloud resources cleanly into logical architectural tiers (Network Ingress & Connectivity $\to$ Compute Workloads $\to$ Backend Data & Storage $\to$ Security & Governance) with balanced multi-column row wrapping.
  - **Connected Clusters**: Physics-based force-directed organic layout clustering resources naturally by their direct topological connections.
  - **Dependency Tree**: Level-ordered hierarchical tree arrangement reflecting deployment and downstream dependencies.
  - **Grid View**: Compact, evenly spaced card matrix for rapid resource inventory inspection.
  - **Fit View Control (`⛶`)**: Instantly auto-fits and centers the entire graph within the container canvas.
  - **Expand / Focus Mode (`⛶`)**: Toggles the canvas to full workspace width by collapsing the findings panel.
  - **Live Wire Traffic Flow (`⚡ Live Traffic`)**: Dynamic animated dashed energy flow showing real-time ingress traffic (Public IP to NIC/App in neon orange) and outgoing data flows (App to Storage, Redis, Database, and Key Vault in emerald green/cyan/purple), with toggleable pause/resume.
- **Azure Infrastructure Visualization**:
  - **Azure-Style Resource Cards**: Generous 230px × 76px cards with official Azure service vector SVG icons (Virtual Machines, VNets, Subnets, NSGs, NICs, Public IPs, Storage Accounts, Key Vaults, Web Apps, SQL Databases, Load Balancers, Application Gateways, Managed Identities, Roles, Automation, Redis, and more).
  - **Visually Differentiated Edges**: Solid and dashed color-coded connections representing deployment dependencies (slate dashed), network links (cyan solid), public IP exposure (orange solid), data flows (emerald green dashed), Key Vault secrets (violet dashed), NSG boundaries (purple dashed), authorization bindings (yellow dotted), app hosting (teal solid), and monitoring destinations (emerald dashed).
  - **Dual Engine Architecture**: Cytoscape.js engine with an automatic, 100% offline Native SVG Topology Visualizer fallback featuring hardware-accelerated animated photon/data packet particles (`<animateMotion>`).
- **Deterministic Security Rule Engine (23 Unique Rules)**:
  - Evaluates 23 unique deterministic security rules spanning Network Security, Storage Security, App Service, Identity & RBAC, Encryption, Secrets, and Logging.
  - Each finding provides severity, category, affected resource, JSON path, evidence snippet, attack impact, and concrete remediation steps.
  - **Separate Metric Reporting**: Uniquely counted rule IDs are tracked and reported separately from total finding instances.
- **Bidirectional Interactivity**:
  - Clicking a finding in the audit list instantly centers, pulses, and highlights the affected node in the topology canvas.
  - Clicking a node opens the Resource Inspector showing configuration properties, risk level, associated findings, and topological links.
- **Exportable Security Reports**:
  - One-click export of the complete security audit report in standard structured JSON format.

---

## 3. Implemented Security Rules Catalog (23 Unique Rules)

| Rule ID | Severity | Category | Rule Title & Purpose | Reference Mapping |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`SEC-NET-001`** | **HIGH** | Network Security | **Unrestricted Inbound SSH Access (Port 22)**: Flags NSG rules allowing port 22 from `*` or `0.0.0.0/0`. | Baseline |
| **`SEC-NET-002`** | **HIGH** | Network Security | **Unrestricted Inbound RDP Access (Port 3389)**: Flags NSG rules allowing port 3389 from `*` or `0.0.0.0/0`. | Baseline |
| **`SEC-NET-003`** | **CRITICAL** | Network Security | **Unrestricted Inbound on All Ports (*)**: Flags NSG rules permitting all protocols/ports from any source. | Baseline |
| **`SEC-NET-004`** | **HIGH** | Network Exposure | **Public IP Assigned Without NSG**: Flags NICs with Public IPs that lack Network Security Group protection. | Baseline |
| **`SEC-NET-005`** | **MEDIUM** | Network Security | **Unrestricted Inbound HTTP Access (Port 80)**: Flags NSG rules allowing cleartext HTTP from internet sources. | Baseline |
| **`SEC-STO-001`** | **HIGH** | Storage Security | **Public Blob Access Allowed**: Flags storage accounts where `allowBlobPublicAccess` is enabled. | Baseline |
| **`SEC-STO-002`** | **HIGH** | Storage Security | **Insecure HTTP Allowed**: Flags storage accounts where `supportsHttpsTrafficOnly` is disabled. | Baseline |
| **`SEC-STO-003`** | **MEDIUM** | Storage Security | **Storage Weak TLS Version**: Flags storage accounts with minimum TLS version omitted or below `TLS1_2`. | Baseline |
| **`SEC-STO-004`** | **LOW** | Storage Security | **Public Network Access Enabled**: Flags storage accounts accessible from all public networks. | Baseline |
| **`SEC-APP-001`** | **HIGH** | App Service | **Remote Debugging Enabled**: Flags Web, API, and Function apps with remote debugging enabled. | `TA-000002`, `000008`, `000014` |
| **`SEC-APP-002`** | **HIGH** | App Service | **FTPS Enforcement Disabled**: Flags App Services where `ftpsState` allows cleartext FTP. | `TA-000003`, `000009`, `000015` |
| **`SEC-APP-003`** | **HIGH** | App Service | **Minimum TLS Version Below 1.2**: Flags App Services allowing legacy TLS 1.0 or 1.1. | `TA-000005`, `000011`, `000017` |
| **`SEC-APP-004`** | **MEDIUM** | App Service | **CORS Allows Wildcard All Origins (*)**: Flags App Services with wildcard `*` allowed origins. | `TA-000006`, `000012`, `000018` |
| **`SEC-APP-005`** | **LOW** | Identity & Access | **Managed Identity Disabled**: Flags App Services that lack a configured Managed Identity. | `TA-000007`, `000013`, `000019` |
| **`SEC-KV-001`** | **HIGH** | Security & Secrets | **Key Vault Soft Delete / Purge Protection Disabled**: Flags Key Vaults lacking deletion protection. | Baseline |
| **`SEC-ID-001`** | **HIGH** | Identity & Access | **Subscription-Scope Owner/Contributor**: Flags role assignments granting privileged roles at root scope. | Baseline |
| **`SEC-ID-002`** | **HIGH** | Identity & Access | **Custom Role Wildcard Actions (*)**: Flags custom role definitions containing wildcard `"*"` permissions. | `TA-000020` |
| **`SEC-ENC-001`** | **MEDIUM** | Encryption & Transit | **App Service HTTPS Disabled**: Flags web apps where `httpsOnly` is false or unset. | `TA-000004`, `000010`, `000016` |
| **`SEC-ENC-002`** | **HIGH** | Encryption & Transit | **SQL Database TDE Disabled**: Flags Azure SQL databases with Transparent Data Encryption disabled. | `TA-000027` |
| **`SEC-ENC-003`** | **LOW** | Encryption & Transit | **Storage Infrastructure Encryption Disabled**: Flags storage accounts lacking secondary encryption. | Baseline |
| **`SEC-AUT-001`** | **HIGH** | Encryption & Secrets | **Automation Variable Asset Unencrypted**: Flags Automation variables created without encryption. | `TA-000021` |
| **`SEC-REDIS-001`**| **HIGH** | Encryption & Transit | **Redis Non-SSL Port Enabled**: Flags Azure Cache for Redis instances with cleartext port 6379 enabled. | `TA-000022` |
| **`SEC-LOG-001`** | **LOW** | Logging & Monitoring | **Missing Diagnostic Settings**: Flags Key Vaults / NSGs deployed without accompanying diagnosticSettings. | `TA-000001` |

> For a complete comparison against all 38 rules from Microsoft's Template Analyzer catalog, see [`docs/security-rule-coverage.md`](docs/security-rule-coverage.md).

## 7. Known Limitations & Static Analysis Boundaries

As an offline, pre-deployment static template analyzer, the engine has defined boundaries:

1. **Unresolved Runtime ARM Expressions**:
   - ARM expressions requiring live Azure ARM context (such as `reference(...)`, `listKeys(...)`, runtime deployment outputs, or `uniqueString(subscription().id)`) cannot be evaluated without an active Azure connection.
   - The engine gracefully tags these constructs as `[Symbolic:<expression>]` without failing and records them in analysis warnings banner.
2. **Linked & External Nested Templates**:
   - Templates referencing external template URIs via `Microsoft.Resources/deployments` (`properties.templateLink.uri`) require out-of-band retrieval. Inline nested templates (`properties.template`) are fully parsed.
3. **Runtime & Directory State**:
   - Security configurations dependent on live tenant state (such as Microsoft Entra ID privileged role assignments, Privileged Identity Management (PIM) policies, Azure Policy initiatives, or cost center quotas) are outside the scope of static Infrastructure-as-Code analysis.
4. **Offline Air-Gapped Operation**:
   - The core deterministic application does not require internet connectivity. Vector SVG icons, fallback layouts, Cytoscape/SVG engines, and deterministic security rules function completely offline.

---

## 8. Phase 2: Dual AI Provider Threat Intelligence & Attack-Path Analysis

Phase 2 introduces an optional AI-assisted threat intelligence layer supporting both cloud-hosted **Google Gemini API** and 100% local, air-gapped **Ollama Local API**.

### Key AI Features:
- **Dual AI Provider Architecture**: Switch seamlessly between Google Gemini API (cloud) and Ollama (local private inference) using `AI_PROVIDER=gemini` or `AI_PROVIDER=ollama`. All API keys, endpoints, and model configurations are strictly imported from the `.env` file without hardcoded dummy defaults.
- **Correlated Attack-Path Modeling**: Identifies entry points (Public IPs, open inbound ports) and maps lateral movement paths toward sensitive target resources (Storage, Redis, SQL Databases, Key Vaults, Privileged Roles).
- **Contextual Finding Explanations (`⚡ AI Explain`)**: Generates architectural context, blast radius analysis, attacker perspective, and verification steps for any finding.
- **Remediation Generator & In-Memory Rescan (`🛠️ AI Fix` & `🔬 Dry-Run Rescan`)**: Generates minimal ARM JSON fix snippets and simulates the fix in-memory to verify that the security violation is eliminated before deployment.
- **Strict Grounding & Anti-Hallucination**: Server-side `AIResponseValidator` validates every cited `resource_id` and `finding_id` against the active session inventory.
- **Prompt-Injection Defense & Secret Masking**: Template properties are isolated inside untrusted data boundaries with credentials/secrets redacted before context construction.
- **Deterministic Cache**: Hash-based caching (`AIResponseCache`) prevents redundant inference spending across re-renders.

---

### Configuration & Switching Providers

Create or update `.env` in the project root (see `.env.example`):

#### Option A: Google Gemini API (Cloud)
```env
AI_ENABLED=true
AI_PROVIDER=gemini

GEMINI_API_KEY=AIzaSy...your_gemini_api_key_here
GEMINI_MODEL=gemini-3.5-flash

AI_REQUEST_TIMEOUT=60
AI_MAX_RETRIES=2
AI_CACHE_ENABLED=true
```
*Note: If `gemini-3.5-flash` is unreleased or returns 404, the system automatically falls back to the recommended supported model `gemini-2.0-flash`.*

#### Option B: Local Ollama (Private / Air-Gapped)
```env
AI_ENABLED=true
AI_PROVIDER=ollama

OLLAMA_BASE_URL=http://localhost:11434
OLLAMA_MODEL=qwen3.5

AI_REQUEST_TIMEOUT=60
AI_MAX_RETRIES=2
AI_CACHE_ENABLED=true
```

To run Ollama locally:
1. Start Ollama: `ollama serve`
2. Pull your chosen model: `ollama pull qwen3.5` (or `llama3.2`, `mistral`, `phi3`)
3. Set `AI_PROVIDER=ollama` in `.env`.

---

```powershell
pytest tests/test_ai_provider.py
pytest tests/
```

FILE PATH: app\__init__.py

import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from flask import Flask, render_template, request, send_from_directory

# Ensure the root package directory is always on sys.path
package_root = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)
if package_root not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, package_root)

from config import config_by_name

def create_app(config_name=None):
    """Application factory for Azure Security Analyzer."""
    if config_name is None:
        config_name = os.environ.get('FLASK_CONFIG', 'default')

    current_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    static_dir = current_dir / 'static'
    template_dir = current_dir / 'templates'

    app = Flask(
        __name__,
        static_folder=str(static_dir),
        template_folder=str(template_dir),
        static_url_path='/static'
    )
    app.config.from_object(config_by_name[config_name])

    # Ensure upload directory exists
    os.makedirs(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], exist_ok=True)

    # Register blueprints
    from app.routes.main import main_bp
    from app.routes.upload import upload_bp
    from app.routes.analysis import analysis_bp
    from app.routes.ai_analysis import ai_bp

    app.register_blueprint(main_bp)
    app.register_blueprint(upload_bp)
    app.register_blueprint(analysis_bp)
    app.register_blueprint(ai_bp)

    # Explicit static route fallback to guarantee correct MIME types
    @app.route('/static/<path:filename>')
    def serve_static(filename):
        return send_from_directory(str(static_dir), filename)

    # Error handlers
    @app.errorhandler(413)
    def request_entity_too_large(error):
        return render_template(
            'index.html',
            error_message="Uploaded file exceeds the maximum allowed file size limit (10 MB)."
        ), 413

    @app.errorhandler(404)
    def page_not_found(error):
        # Don't serve HTML on missing static assets or API endpoints
        if request.path.startswith('/static/') or request.path.startswith('/api/'):
            return ("Asset or endpoint not found", 404, {'Content-Type': 'text/plain'})
        return render_template('index.html', error_message="The requested page or analysis session was not found."), 404

    @app.errorhandler(500)
    def internal_server_error(error):
        return render_template('index.html', error_message="An unexpected server error occurred during processing."), 500

    return app

import json
import traceback
from flask import Blueprint, jsonify, request, current_app
from app.routes.analysis import _load_and_analyze
from app.services.ai import get_ai_provider, get_ai_cache
from app.services.ai.threat_modeler import ThreatModeler
from app.services.ai.finding_explainer import FindingExplainer
from app.services.ai.risk_prioritizer import RiskPrioritizer
from app.services.ai.remediation_generator import RemediationGenerator
from app.utils.sanitization import get_storage_path
from app.services.ai.provider import (
    AIProviderError,
    AIAuthenticationError,
    AIRateLimitError,
    AIQuotaExceededError,
    AITimeoutError,
    AIValidationError,
    AIModelNotFoundError,
    AIConnectionError,
)

ai_bp = Blueprint('ai', __name__)

@ai_bp.route('/api/ai/status', methods=['GET'])
def ai_status():
    """Returns AI engine availability and provider configuration status."""
    provider = get_ai_provider()
    if not provider:
        return jsonify({
            "enabled": False,
            "status": "disabled",
            "message": "AI analysis is disabled or unconfigured."
        })

    health = provider.health_check()
    health["enabled"] = True
    return jsonify(health)

@ai_bp.route('/api/ai/threat-model/<file_id>', methods=['POST'])
def analyze_threat_model(file_id: str):
    """Generates or retrieves cached AI attack-path analysis for the given session."""
    analysis_data = _load_and_analyze(file_id)
    if not analysis_data:
        return jsonify({
            "success": False,
            "error": "Analysis session not found or expired.",
            "code": "NOT_FOUND"
        }), 404

    provider = get_ai_provider()
    if not provider:
        return jsonify({
            "success": False,
            "error": "AI provider is not configured. Set AI_PROVIDER in your environment.",
            "code": "AI_UNCONFIGURED"
        }), 400

    health = provider.health_check()
    if health.get("status") in ("unconfigured", "unavailable", "model_missing"):
        provider_name = health.get("provider", "ai")
        err_msg = health.get("error", f"{provider_name.capitalize()} provider is not ready.")
        return jsonify({
            "success": False,
            "error": err_msg,
            "code": "AI_UNCONFIGURED" if health.get("status") == "unconfigured" else "AI_UNAVAILABLE",
            "provider": provider_name,
            "details": health
        }), 400

    cache = get_ai_cache()
    force_refresh = request.args.get("refresh", "false").lower() == "true"

    try:
        modeler = ThreatModeler(provider=provider, cache=cache)
        result = modeler.analyze_attack_paths(
            file_id=file_id,
            resources=analysis_data["resources"],
            findings=analysis_data["findings"],
            edges=analysis_data["graph"].get("edges", []),
            summary=analysis_data.get("summary"),
            warnings=analysis_data.get("warnings"),
            force_refresh=force_refresh
        )

        return jsonify({
            "success": True,
            "file_id": file_id,
            "provider": provider.model_name,
            "cached": result.get("cached", False),
            "total_paths": result.get("total_paths", 0),
            "attack_paths": result.get("attack_paths", [])
        })

    except AIModelNotFoundError as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": str(e), "code": "MODEL_NOT_FOUND"}), 404
    except AIConnectionError as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": str(e), "code": "CONNECTION_FAILED"}), 503
    except AIAuthenticationError as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": str(e), "code": "AUTH_FAILED"}), 401
    except AIQuotaExceededError as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": str(e), "code": "QUOTA_EXCEEDED"}), 429
    except AIRateLimitError as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": str(e), "code": "RATE_LIMITED"}), 429
    except AITimeoutError as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": str(e), "code": "TIMEOUT"}), 504
    except AIValidationError as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": f"AI response validation failed: {str(e)}", "code": "VALIDATION_FAILED"}), 422
    except AIProviderError as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": f"AI provider error: {str(e)}", "code": "PROVIDER_ERROR"}), 502
    except Exception as e:
        traceback.print_exc()
        current_app.logger.error(f"AI Threat Modeling failed: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": f"An unexpected error occurred during AI threat modeling: {str(e)}", "code": "INTERNAL_ERROR"}), 500

@ai_bp.route('/api/ai/explain/<file_id>/<finding_id>', methods=['POST'])
def explain_finding(file_id: str, finding_id: str):
    """Generates or retrieves contextual architectural explanation for a single finding."""
    analysis_data = _load_and_analyze(file_id)
    if not analysis_data:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": "Session not found.", "code": "NOT_FOUND"}), 404

    provider = get_ai_provider()
    if not provider:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": "AI provider unconfigured.", "code": "AI_UNCONFIGURED"}), 400

    target_finding = next((f for f in analysis_data["findings"] if f.get("finding_id") == finding_id), None)
    if not target_finding:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": "Finding not found in active session.", "code": "FINDING_NOT_FOUND"}), 404

    target_res_id = target_finding.get("affected_resource_id")
    target_res = next((r for r in analysis_data["resources"] if r.get("id") == target_res_id), None)

    cache = get_ai_cache()
    force_refresh = request.args.get("refresh", "false").lower() == "true"

    try:
        explainer = FindingExplainer(provider=provider, cache=cache)
        explanation = explainer.explain_finding(
            file_id=file_id,
            finding_id=finding_id,
            finding=target_finding,
            target_resource=target_res,
            related_resources=analysis_data["resources"],
            related_findings=analysis_data["findings"],
            force_refresh=force_refresh
        )
        return jsonify({"success": True, "explanation": explanation})
    except AIProviderError as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": str(e), "code": "PROVIDER_ERROR"}), 502
    except Exception as e:
        traceback.print_exc()
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": str(e), "code": "INTERNAL_ERROR"}), 500

@ai_bp.route('/api/ai/remediate/<file_id>/<finding_id>', methods=['POST'])

        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": "AI provider unconfigured.", "code": "AI_UNCONFIGURED"}), 400

        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": "Finding not found.", "code": "FINDING_NOT_FOUND"}), 404

    target_res_id = target_finding.get("affected_resource_id")
    target_res = next((r for r in analysis_data["resources"] if r.get("id") == target_res_id), None)

    cache = get_ai_cache()
    force_refresh = request.args.get("refresh", "false").lower() == "true"

            file_id=file_id,
            finding_id=finding_id,
            finding=target_finding,
            target_resource=target_res,
            force_refresh=force_refresh
        )
        return jsonify({"success": True, "remediation": result})
    except AIProviderError as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": str(e), "code": "PROVIDER_ERROR"}), 502
    except Exception as e:
        traceback.print_exc()
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": str(e), "code": "INTERNAL_ERROR"}), 500

@ai_bp.route('/api/ai/remediate/<file_id>/<finding_id>/rescan', methods=['POST'])
def dry_run_rescan(file_id: str, finding_id: str):
    """Dry-runs proposed patch in memory and validates that the security violation is eliminated."""
    analysis_data = _load_and_analyze(file_id)
    if not analysis_data:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": "Session not found.", "code": "NOT_FOUND"}), 404

    target_finding = next((f for f in analysis_data["findings"] if f.get("finding_id") == finding_id), None)
    if not target_finding:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": "Finding not found.", "code": "FINDING_NOT_FOUND"}), 404

    payload = request.get_json() or {}
    patch_properties = payload.get("patch_properties", {})

    upload_dir = current_app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER']
    try:
        file_path = get_storage_path(upload_dir, file_id)
        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            original_template = json.load(f)
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": f"Failed reading template: {str(e)}", "code": "FILE_READ_ERROR"}), 500

    provider = get_ai_provider()
    generator = RemediationGenerator(provider=provider or ThreatModeler)
    verification = generator.dry_run_rescan(
        original_template_data=original_template,
        target_resource_id=target_finding.get("affected_resource_id"),
        target_rule_id=target_finding.get("rule_id"),
        patch_properties=patch_properties
    )

    return jsonify({"success": True, "verification": verification})

import os
import json
from pathlib import Path
from flask import Blueprint, render_template, jsonify, abort, current_app, Response, redirect, url_for, flash
from app.utils.sanitization import get_storage_path, generate_secure_storage_id
from app.services.template_parser import ArmTemplateParser
from app.services.resource_mapper import ResourceMapper
from app.services.security_scanner import SecurityScanner
from app.services.report_generator import ReportGenerator

analysis_bp = Blueprint('analysis', __name__)

def _load_and_analyze(file_id: str):
    """Internal helper to load template, parse, scan, map graph, and generate report."""
    upload_dir = current_app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER']
    try:
        file_path = get_storage_path(upload_dir, file_id)
    except ValueError:
        return None

    if not file_path.exists():
        return None

    # Load metadata if exists
    meta_path = Path(upload_dir) / f"{file_id}.meta.json"
    filename = "template.json"
    upload_time = ""
    meta_warnings = []
    if meta_path.exists():
        try:
            with open(meta_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as mf:
                meta = json.load(mf)
                filename = meta.get("original_filename", filename)
                upload_time = meta.get("upload_timestamp", "")
                meta_warnings = meta.get("validation_warnings", [])
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Read template content
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            template_data = json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return None

    # 1. Parse ARM template
    parser = ArmTemplateParser(template_data)
    resources, metadata, parse_warnings = parser.parse()
    all_warnings = meta_warnings + parse_warnings

    # 2. Run deterministic security rules
    scanner = SecurityScanner()
    findings = scanner.scan(resources, metadata)

    # 3. Build Cytoscape graph
    mapper = ResourceMapper(resources, findings)
    graph_data = mapper.generate_graph()

    # 4. Generate report summary
    reporter = ReportGenerator(
        file_id=file_id,
        filename=filename,
        resources=resources,
        edges=graph_data["edges"],
        findings=findings,
        metadata=metadata,
        warnings=all_warnings
    )
    summary = reporter.generate_summary()
    export_data = reporter.generate_export_report()

    return {
        "file_id": file_id,
        "filename": filename,
        "upload_time": upload_time,
        "summary": summary,
        "graph": graph_data,
        "findings": [f.to_dict() for f in findings],
        "resources": [
            {
                "id": r.id,
                "name": r.name,
                "type": r.type,
                "category": r.category,
                "location": r.location,
                "api_version": r.api_version,
                "depends_on": r.depends_on,
                "json_path": r.json_path,
                "properties": r.properties
            }
            for r in resources
        ],
        "export_data": export_data,
        "warnings": all_warnings
    }

@analysis_bp.route('/analysis/<file_id>')
def view_analysis(file_id: str):
    """Renders the interactive visualizer and security dashboard."""
    analysis_data = _load_and_analyze(file_id)
    if not analysis_data:
        flash(f"Analysis session '{file_id}' not found or expired.", "error")
        return redirect(url_for('main.index'))

    return render_template(
        'dashboard.html',
        file_id=file_id,
        filename=analysis_data["filename"],
        summary=analysis_data["summary"],
        initial_data_json=json.dumps({
            "graph": analysis_data["graph"],
            "findings": analysis_data["findings"],
            "resources": analysis_data["resources"],
            "summary": analysis_data["summary"],
            "warnings": analysis_data["warnings"]
        })
    )

@analysis_bp.route('/api/analysis/<file_id>')
def get_analysis_data(file_id: str):
    """API endpoint providing the graph and findings JSON payload."""
    analysis_data = _load_and_analyze(file_id)
    if not analysis_data:
        return jsonify({"error": "Template analysis not found"}), 404

    return jsonify({
        "success": True,
        "file_id": file_id,
        "filename": analysis_data["filename"],
        "summary": analysis_data["summary"],
        "graph": analysis_data["graph"],
        "findings": analysis_data["findings"],
        "resources": analysis_data["resources"],
        "warnings": analysis_data["warnings"]
    })

@analysis_bp.route('/report/<file_id>/export')
def export_report(file_id: str):
    """Exports the complete audit report as a downloadable JSON file."""
    analysis_data = _load_and_analyze(file_id)
    if not analysis_data:
        abort(404, description="Template analysis not found")

    report_json = json.dumps(analysis_data["export_data"], indent=2)
    response = Response(report_json, mimetype='application/json')
    response.headers['Content-Disposition'] = f'attachment; filename=security-report-{file_id[:8]}.json'
    return response

@analysis_bp.route('/api/load-sample/<sample_name>', methods=['POST'])
def load_sample(sample_name: str):
    """Quickly loads a pre-packaged sample template for immediate demonstration."""
    sample_files = {
        "secure": "secure_template.json",
        "insecure": "insecure_template.json",
        "complex": "complex_template.json"
    }

    if sample_name not in sample_files:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": f"Unknown sample '{sample_name}'"}), 400

    base_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
    sample_path = base_dir / "tests" / "fixtures" / sample_files[sample_name]

    if not sample_path.exists():
        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": f"Sample fixture file not found: {sample_files[sample_name]}"}), 404

    with open(sample_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        content_str = f.read()

    upload_dir = current_app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER']
    os.makedirs(upload_dir, exist_ok=True)
    file_id = generate_secure_storage_id()
    dest_path = get_storage_path(upload_dir, file_id)

    with open(dest_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(content_str)

    meta_path = Path(upload_dir) / f"{file_id}.meta.json"
    with open(meta_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json.dump({
            "file_id": file_id,
            "original_filename": f"sample_{sample_name}.json",
            "upload_timestamp": "sample-demo",
            "validation_status": "VALID",
            "validation_warnings": []
        }, f, indent=2)

    return jsonify({
        "success": True,
        "file_id": file_id,
        "redirect_url": url_for('analysis.view_analysis', file_id=file_id)
    })

@main_bp.route('/sw.js')
def service_worker():
    """Unregisters any stale development service worker previously registered on 127.0.0.1."""
    content = "self.addEventListener('install', () => self.skipWaiting()); self.addEventListener('activate', () => self.registration.unregister());"

import os
import json
import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, redirect, url_for, flash, current_app
from werkzeug.utils import secure_filename
from app.utils.validation import allowed_file, validate_arm_content
from app.utils.sanitization import generate_secure_storage_id, get_storage_path

upload_bp = Blueprint('upload', __name__)

@upload_bp.route('/upload', methods=['POST'])
def upload_file():
    """Handles ARM template file upload and validation."""
    is_ajax = request.is_json or request.headers.get('X-Requested-With') == 'XMLHttpRequest'

    if 'template_file' not in request.files:
        if is_ajax:
            return jsonify({"success": False, "error": "No file field 'template_file' provided."}), 400
        flash("No file was selected for upload.", "error")
        return redirect(url_for('main.index'))

    file = request.files['template_file']

    if file.filename == '':
        if is_ajax:
            return jsonify({"success": False, "error": "No file selected."}), 400
        flash("No file was selected for upload.", "error")
        return redirect(url_for('main.index'))

    allowed_exts = current_app.config.get('ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS', {'json'})
    if not allowed_file(file.filename, allowed_exts):
        msg = f"Invalid file type '{file.filename}'. Only ARM JSON templates (.json) are permitted."
        if is_ajax:
            return jsonify({"success": False, "error": msg}), 400
        flash(msg, "error")
        return redirect(url_for('main.index'))

    try:
        content_bytes = file.read()
        content_str = content_bytes.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
    except Exception as e:
        msg = f"Could not read uploaded file: {str(e)}"
        if is_ajax:
            return jsonify({"success": False, "error": msg}), 400
        flash(msg, "error")
        return redirect(url_for('main.index'))

    # Validate ARM structure
    is_valid, status, parsed_data, warnings_or_errors = validate_arm_content(content_str)
    if not is_valid:
        error_msg = " | ".join(warnings_or_errors)
        if is_ajax:
            return jsonify({
                "success": False,
                "status": status,
                "error": error_msg,
                "details": warnings_or_errors
            }), 400
        flash(f"Template validation failed ({status}): {error_msg}", "error")
        return redirect(url_for('main.index'))

    # Store validated template securely
    upload_dir = current_app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER']
    os.makedirs(upload_dir, exist_ok=True)
    file_id = generate_secure_storage_id()
    dest_path = get_storage_path(upload_dir, file_id)

    with open(dest_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(content_str)

    # Store metadata alongside
    meta_path = Path(upload_dir) / f"{file_id}.meta.json"
    with open(meta_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json.dump({
            "file_id": file_id,
            "original_filename": secure_filename(file.filename),
            "upload_timestamp": datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z"),
            "validation_status": status,
            "validation_warnings": warnings_or_errors
        }, f, indent=2)

    redirect_target = url_for('analysis.view_analysis', file_id=file_id)
    if is_ajax:
        return jsonify({
            "success": True,
            "file_id": file_id,
            "status": status,
            "warnings": warnings_or_errors,
            "redirect_url": redirect_target
        })

    return redirect(redirect_target)

================================================================================
FILE PATH: app\security_rules\app_service_rules.py

import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional, Tuple
from app.security_rules.base_rule import BaseSecurityRule, SecurityFinding
from app.services.template_parser import NormalizedResource

def extract_app_config(resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource]) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], str]:
    # Check inline siteConfig
    inline_config = resource.properties.get("siteConfig")
    if isinstance(inline_config, dict):
        return inline_config, f"{resource.json_path}.properties.siteConfig"

    # Check child/associated config resources
    res_name_lower = resource.name.lower()
    res_id_lower = resource.id.lower()
    for other in all_resources:
        if other.type.lower() == "microsoft.web/sites/config":
            if other.name.lower() in ("web", "appsettings") or res_name_lower in other.id.lower() or res_id_lower in other.id.lower():
                return other.properties, other.json_path
            # Check dependsOn or parent
            if any(res_name_lower in dep.lower() or res_id_lower in dep.lower() for dep in other.depends_on):
                return other.properties, other.json_path

    # Default to direct properties on site if present
    return resource.properties, f"{resource.json_path}.properties"


class AppServiceRemoteDebuggingRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    """
    TA-000002, TA-000008, TA-000014: Remote debugging should be turned off for API, Function, and Web apps.
    """
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-APP-001"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "App Service Remote Debugging Enabled"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Remote debugging opens diagnostic inbound ports on App Services, exposing them to internet-based attacks and compromise."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "HIGH"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "App Service"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.web/sites"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        config, path_prefix = extract_app_config(resource, all_resources)
        remote_debug = config.get("remoteDebuggingEnabled")

        if remote_debug is True or str(remote_debug).lower() == "true":
            findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                rule_id=self.rule_id,
                title=self.title,
                description=f"App Service '{resource.name}' has remote debugging enabled.",
                severity=self.severity,
                category=self.category,
                affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                json_path=f"{path_prefix}.remoteDebuggingEnabled",
                evidence="remoteDebuggingEnabled is explicitly set to true.",
                impact="Remote debugging opens listening debug ports that bypass standard authentication, enabling remote code execution and data exfiltration.",
                remediation="Set 'remoteDebuggingEnabled' to false or remove the property from the App Service siteConfig."
            ))
        return findings


class AppServiceFtpsEnforcementRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    """
    TA-000003, TA-000009, TA-000015: FTPS only should be required in your App Service.
    """
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-APP-002"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "App Service Allows Insecure FTP Access"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "FTP without TLS encrypts neither credentials nor application code in transit. App Services must enforce FTPS only or disable FTP entirely."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "HIGH"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "App Service"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.web/sites"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        config, path_prefix = extract_app_config(resource, all_resources)
        ftps_state = config.get("ftpsState")

        # Allowed secure states: FtpsOnly or Disabled
        if ftps_state is not None:
            state_str = str(ftps_state).strip().lower()
            if state_str not in ("ftpsonly", "disabled"):
                findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                    finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                    rule_id=self.rule_id,
                    title=self.title,
                    description=f"App Service '{resource.name}' allows unencrypted FTP (ftpsState: '{ftps_state}').",
                    severity=self.severity,
                    category=self.category,
                    affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                    affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                    affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                    json_path=f"{path_prefix}.ftpsState",
                    evidence=f"ftpsState is configured as '{ftps_state}'.",
                    impact="Cleartext FTP deployment credentials and application source files can be sniffed or intercepted over untrusted networks.",
                    remediation="Configure 'ftpsState' to 'FtpsOnly' or 'Disabled' in siteConfig."
                ))
        return findings


class AppServiceMinTlsVersionRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    """
    TA-000005, TA-000011, TA-000017: Latest TLS version (1.2) should be used in App Services.
    """
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-APP-003"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "App Service Minimum TLS Version Below 1.2"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Legacy TLS versions (1.0 and 1.1) have known protocol vulnerabilities such as POODLE and BEAST. App Services should enforce TLS 1.2."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "HIGH"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "App Service"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.web/sites"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        config, path_prefix = extract_app_config(resource, all_resources)
        min_tls = config.get("minTlsVersion")

        if min_tls is not None:
            tls_str = str(min_tls).strip().lower()
            if tls_str in ("1.0", "1.1", "tls1_0", "tls1_1"):
                findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                    finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                    rule_id=self.rule_id,
                    title=self.title,
                    description=f"App Service '{resource.name}' allows weak TLS version '{min_tls}'.",
                    severity=self.severity,
                    category=self.category,
                    affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                    affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                    affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                    json_path=f"{path_prefix}.minTlsVersion",
                    evidence=f"minTlsVersion is set to '{min_tls}'.",
                    impact="Clients connecting to the web app can negotiate weak cryptographic ciphers vulnerable to decryption and man-in-the-middle attacks.",
                    remediation="Set 'minTlsVersion' to '1.2' in the App Service siteConfig."
                ))
        return findings


class AppServiceCorsWildcardRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    """
    TA-000006, TA-000012, TA-000018: CORS should not allow every origin to access the App Service.
    """
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-APP-004"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "App Service CORS Allows Wildcard All Origins (*)"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Cross-Origin Resource Sharing (CORS) should restrict domain access. Setting allowedOrigins to '*' permits arbitrary malicious web domains to read API responses."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "MEDIUM"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "App Service"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.web/sites"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        config, path_prefix = extract_app_config(resource, all_resources)
        cors = config.get("cors") or {}
        allowed_origins = cors.get("allowedOrigins", [])

        if isinstance(allowed_origins, list) and "*" in allowed_origins:
            findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                rule_id=self.rule_id,
                title=self.title,
                description=f"App Service '{resource.name}' has wildcard '*' in CORS allowedOrigins.",
                severity=self.severity,
                category=self.category,
                affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                json_path=f"{path_prefix}.cors.allowedOrigins",
                evidence="allowedOrigins contains wildcard '*'.",
                impact="Malicious websites visited by authenticated users can make cross-origin requests to this service and exfiltrate confidential data.",
                remediation="Replace wildcard '*' in 'cors.allowedOrigins' with explicit, trusted origin domains."
            ))
        return findings


class AppServiceManagedIdentityRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    """
    TA-000007, TA-000013, TA-000019: Managed identity should be used in App Services.
    """
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-APP-005"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "App Service Managed Identity Disabled"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "App Services should utilize Azure Managed Identities (SystemAssigned or UserAssigned) to avoid embedding hardcoded credentials in connection strings."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "LOW"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Identity & Access"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.web/sites"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        identity = resource.raw_json.get("identity") or resource.properties.get("identity")
        identity_type = ""
        if isinstance(identity, dict):
            identity_type = str(identity.get("type", "")).strip()

        valid_types = {"systemassigned", "userassigned", "systemassigned, userassigned", "systemassigned,userassigned"}
        if not identity_type or identity_type.lower() not in valid_types:
            findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                rule_id=self.rule_id,
                title=self.title,
                description=f"App Service '{resource.name}' does not configure a Managed Identity.",
                severity=self.severity,
                category=self.category,
                affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                json_path=f"{resource.json_path}.identity",
                evidence=f"identity is '{identity_type or 'None'}'.",
                impact="Applications without Managed Identity often rely on static passwords, access keys, or API tokens stored insecurely in configuration files.",
                remediation="Configure 'identity.type' to 'SystemAssigned' or 'UserAssigned' on the App Service site definition."
            ))
        return findings


================================================================================
FILE PATH: app\security_rules\base_rule.py
================================================================================
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from app.services.template_parser import NormalizedResource

@dataclass
class SecurityFinding:
    finding_id: str
    rule_id: str
    title: str
    description: str
    severity: str                   # 'CRITICAL', 'HIGH', 'MEDIUM', 'LOW', 'INFORMATIONAL'
    category: str                   # 'Network Security', 'Storage Security', 'Identity & Access', 'Encryption', 'Logging & Monitoring'
    affected_resource_id: str
    affected_resource_name: str
    affected_resource_type: str
    json_path: str
    evidence: str
    impact: str
    remediation: str
    confidence: str = "Direct"      # 'Direct' or 'Incomplete Evidence'

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)


class BaseSecurityRule(ABC):
    """Abstract base class for all deterministic Azure security rules."""

    @property
    @abstractmethod
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        """Unique rule identifier, e.g. SEC-NET-001."""
        pass

    @property
    @abstractmethod
    def title(self) -> str:
        """Brief descriptive title."""
        pass

    @property
    @abstractmethod
    def description(self) -> str:
        """Detailed description of the check."""
        pass

    @property
    @abstractmethod
    def severity(self) -> str:
        """Finding severity."""
        pass

    @property
    @abstractmethod
    def category(self) -> str:
        """Category of rule."""
        pass

    @property
    @abstractmethod
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        """List of Azure resource types this rule applies to."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        """
        Evaluate this rule against a resource.
        Returns a list of SecurityFinding objects if violations are detected.
        """
        pass

import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from app.security_rules.base_rule import BaseSecurityRule, SecurityFinding
from app.services.template_parser import NormalizedResource

class AppServiceHttpsOnlyRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-ENC-001"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "App Service Web App Allows Unencrypted HTTP"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Azure App Service web applications should enforce HTTPS-only traffic to secure communication in transit."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "MEDIUM"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Encryption & Transit"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.web/sites"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        https_only = resource.properties.get("httpsOnly")
        if https_only is False or str(https_only).lower() == "false" or https_only is None:
            findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                rule_id=self.rule_id,
                title=self.title,
                description=f"Web App '{resource.name}' does not enforce HTTPS-only traffic.",
                severity=self.severity,
                category=self.category,
                affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                json_path=f"{resource.json_path}.properties.httpsOnly",
                evidence=f"httpsOnly is set to '{https_only}'",
                impact="Unencrypted HTTP requests can leak sensitive tokens, cookies, and user credentials in transit.",
                remediation="Configure 'properties.httpsOnly' to true on the App Service site definition."
            ))
        return findings


class SqlDatabaseTdeRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-ENC-002"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "SQL Database Transparent Data Encryption (TDE) Disabled"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Azure SQL Databases should have Transparent Data Encryption enabled to protect data at rest."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "HIGH"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Encryption & Transit"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return [
            "microsoft.sql/servers/databases",
            "microsoft.sql/servers/databases/transparentdataencryption"
        ]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        if resource.type.lower() == "microsoft.sql/servers/databases/transparentdataencryption":
            status = str(resource.properties.get("status", "")).lower()
            if status == "disabled":
                findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                    finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                    rule_id=self.rule_id,
                    title=self.title,
                    description=f"Transparent Data Encryption is explicitly disabled on '{resource.name}'.",
                    severity=self.severity,
                    category=self.category,
                    affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                    affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                    affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                    json_path=f"{resource.json_path}.properties.status",
                    evidence="transparentDataEncryption status is 'Disabled'",
                    impact="Database data files and backups are stored unencrypted at the storage level.",
                    remediation="Set 'properties.status' to 'Enabled' in the transparentDataEncryption resource."
                ))
        return findings

                remediation="Enable 'requireInfrastructureEncryption: true' for workloads with strict compliance standards.",
                confidence="Direct"
            ))

import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from app.security_rules.base_rule import BaseSecurityRule, SecurityFinding
from app.services.template_parser import NormalizedResource

OWNER_ROLE_ID = "8e3af657-a8ff-443c-a75c-2fe8c4bcb635"
CONTRIBUTOR_ROLE_ID = "b24988ac-6180-42a0-ab88-20f7382dd24c"

class BroadSubscriptionRoleAssignmentRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-ID-001"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "High-Privilege Role Assigned at Subscription Scope"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Role assignments granting Owner or Contributor at the subscription scope violate the principle of least privilege."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "HIGH"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Identity & Access"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.authorization/roleassignments"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        role_def = str(resource.properties.get("roleDefinitionId", "")).lower()
        scope = str(resource.properties.get("scope", "")).lower()

        is_high_priv = OWNER_ROLE_ID in role_def or CONTRIBUTOR_ROLE_ID in role_def or "owner" in role_def or "contributor" in role_def
        is_broad_scope = not scope or scope in ("/", "[subscription().id]", "subscription()") or (scope.startswith("/subscriptions/") and "/resourcegroups/" not in scope)

        if is_high_priv and is_broad_scope:
            role_label = "Owner" if (OWNER_ROLE_ID in role_def or "owner" in role_def) else "Contributor"
            findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                rule_id=self.rule_id,
                title=self.title,
                description=f"Role assignment '{resource.name}' assigns broad '{role_label}' role across the entire subscription scope.",
                severity=self.severity,
                category=self.category,
                affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                json_path=f"{resource.json_path}.properties.scope",
                evidence=f"roleDefinitionId: '{role_def}', scope: '{scope or 'Subscription level'}'",
                impact="If the assigned identity is compromised, the attacker gains full control over all cloud resources in the subscription.",
                remediation="Scope the role assignment to a specific Resource Group or resource, and use custom or specific roles with minimal required permissions."
            ))
        return findings


class CustomRoleWildcardRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-ID-002"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "Custom Role Definition Contains Wildcard Actions (*)"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Custom RBAC role definitions should not include wildcard actions '*' which grant unconstrained permissions."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "HIGH"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Identity & Access"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.authorization/roledefinitions"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        permissions = resource.properties.get("permissions", [])
        if isinstance(permissions, list):
            for idx, perm in enumerate(permissions):
                actions = perm.get("actions", [])
                if isinstance(actions, list) and any(a == "*" or a.endswith("/*") for a in actions):
                    findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                        finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                        rule_id=self.rule_id,
                        title=self.title,
                        description=f"Custom role '{resource.name}' contains wildcard actions '{actions}'.",
                        severity=self.severity,
                        category=self.category,
                        affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                        affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                        affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                        json_path=f"{resource.json_path}.properties.permissions[{idx}].actions",
                        evidence=f"Permissions contain wildcard: {actions}",
                        impact="Grants excessive actions across current and future Azure resource provider operations, leading to privilege escalation.",
                        remediation="Explicitly enumerate only the specific Azure operations (e.g. 'Microsoft.Storage/storageAccounts/read') needed by the workload."
                    ))
        return findings

import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from app.security_rules.base_rule import BaseSecurityRule, SecurityFinding
from app.services.template_parser import NormalizedResource

class KeyVaultPurgeProtectionRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    """
    Key Vault soft delete and purge protection should be enabled to prevent accidental
    or malicious permanent deletion of secrets, keys, and certificates.
    """
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-KV-001"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "Key Vault Soft Delete or Purge Protection Disabled"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Azure Key Vaults must enable soft delete and purge protection to safeguard cryptographic keys and secrets against ransomware and unrecoverable deletion."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "HIGH"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Security & Secrets"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.keyvault/vaults"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        enable_soft_delete = resource.properties.get("enableSoftDelete")
        enable_purge_protection = resource.properties.get("enablePurgeProtection")

        is_soft_disabled = (enable_soft_delete is False or str(enable_soft_delete).lower() == "false")
        is_purge_disabled = (enable_purge_protection is False or str(enable_purge_protection).lower() == "false")

        if is_soft_disabled or is_purge_disabled:
            findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                rule_id=self.rule_id,
                title=self.title,
                description=f"Key Vault '{resource.name}' does not enforce soft-delete or purge protection.",
                severity=self.severity,
                category=self.category,
                affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                json_path=f"{resource.json_path}.properties",
                evidence=f"enableSoftDelete: {enable_soft_delete}, enablePurgeProtection: {enable_purge_protection}",
                impact="If deleted by a compromised admin account or ransomware script, cryptographic keys and production certificates are permanently lost without recovery.",
                remediation="Set 'properties.enableSoftDelete' to true and 'properties.enablePurgeProtection' to true on Key Vault resources."
            ))
        return findings



================================================================================
FILE PATH: app\security_rules\logging_rules.py
================================================================================
import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from app.security_rules.base_rule import BaseSecurityRule, SecurityFinding
from app.services.template_parser import NormalizedResource

MONITORED_SECURITY_TYPES = {
    "microsoft.keyvault/vaults": "Key Vault",
    "microsoft.network/networksecuritygroups": "Network Security Group"
}

class MissingDiagnosticSettingsRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-LOG-001"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "Missing Diagnostic Settings for Security-Sensitive Resource"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Security-sensitive resources such as Key Vaults and NSGs should forward audit and activity logs to Log Analytics or a SIEM."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "LOW"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Logging & Monitoring"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return list(MONITORED_SECURITY_TYPES.keys())

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        r_type = resource.type.lower()
        if r_type not in MONITORED_SECURITY_TYPES:
            return findings

        # Check if there is an accompanying Microsoft.Insights/diagnosticSettings resource targeting this resource
        diag_found = False
        for other in all_resources:
            if other.type.lower() in ("microsoft.insights/diagnosticsettings", "providers/microsoft.insights/diagnosticsettings"):
                # Check if other depends on this or scopes to this
                if resource.id.lower() in [d.lower() for d in other.depends_on] or resource.name.lower() in other.name.lower():
                    diag_found = True
                    break

        if not diag_found:
            friendly_name = MONITORED_SECURITY_TYPES[r_type]
            findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                rule_id=self.rule_id,
                title=f"{friendly_name} Lacks Diagnostic Logging Configuration in Template",
                description=f"{friendly_name} '{resource.name}' does not have an accompanying diagnosticSettings resource defined.",
                severity=self.severity,
                category=self.category,
                affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                json_path=resource.json_path,
                evidence=f"No Microsoft.Insights/diagnosticSettings resource targets {resource.name} in this template.",
                impact="Security incidents, unauthorized secret access, or firewall drop events cannot be investigated without audit trails.",
                remediation="Declare a Microsoft.Insights/diagnosticSettings resource in the template sending audit logs to a Log Analytics Workspace.",
                confidence="Incomplete Evidence"
            ))
        return findings

import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from app.security_rules.base_rule import BaseSecurityRule, SecurityFinding
from app.services.template_parser import NormalizedResource

UNRESTRICTED_SOURCES = {"*", "0.0.0.0/0", "internet", "<any>"}

def is_unrestricted_source(source_val: Any) -> bool:
    if isinstance(source_val, str) and source_val.strip().lower() in UNRESTRICTED_SOURCES:
        return True
    if isinstance(source_val, list):
        return any(isinstance(s, str) and s.strip().lower() in UNRESTRICTED_SOURCES for s in source_val)
    return False

def matches_port(port_val: Any, target_port: str) -> bool:
    if isinstance(port_val, str):
        p_clean = port_val.strip()
        if p_clean == target_port or p_clean == "*":
            return True
        if "-" in p_clean:
            try:
                start, end = map(int, p_clean.split("-"))
                t_port = int(target_port)
                return start <= t_port <= end
            except ValueError:
                pass
    elif isinstance(port_val, list):
        return any(matches_port(p, target_port) for p in port_val)
    return False

def get_nsg_rules(resource: NormalizedResource) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    rules = []
    # In properties.securityRules
    sec_rules = resource.properties.get("securityRules", [])
    if isinstance(sec_rules, list):
        for idx, r in enumerate(sec_rules):
            r_props = r.get("properties", r)
            rules.append((f"{resource.json_path}.properties.securityRules[{idx}]", r_props, r.get("name", f"rule_{idx}")))
    return rules

class NSGOpenSSHRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-NET-001"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "Unrestricted Inbound SSH Access (Port 22)"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Network Security Group allows inbound SSH traffic (port 22) from the internet (0.0.0.0/0 or *)."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "HIGH"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Network Security"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.network/networksecuritygroups"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        for path, props, rule_name in get_nsg_rules(resource):
            direction = str(props.get("direction", "")).lower()
            access = str(props.get("access", "")).lower()
            source = props.get("sourceAddressPrefix") or props.get("sourceAddressPrefixes")
            dest_port = props.get("destinationPortRange") or props.get("destinationPortRanges")

            if direction == "inbound" and access == "allow":
                if is_unrestricted_source(source) and matches_port(dest_port, "22"):
                    findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                        finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                        rule_id=self.rule_id,
                        title=self.title,
                        description=f"NSG rule '{rule_name}' permits inbound SSH (port 22) from '{source}'.",
                        severity=self.severity,
                        category=self.category,
                        affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                        affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                        affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                        json_path=path,
                        evidence=f"Rule: {rule_name}, Access: Allow, Direction: Inbound, Source: {source}, Port: {dest_port}",
                        impact="Exposing SSH to the public internet enables brute force, password spraying, and unauthorized remote code execution attacks.",
                        remediation="Restrict sourceAddressPrefix to specific enterprise IP CIDRs, or deploy Azure Bastion / VPN gateway instead of direct SSH."
                    ))
        return findings

                        finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),

                        impact="Complete bypass of network perimeter defenses exposing all internal host services and application ports.",
                        remediation="Delete this rule and implement the principle of least privilege, allowing only necessary ports and restricted IP ranges."
                    ))
        return findings


class UnprotectedPublicIPRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-NET-004"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "Public IP Associated With NIC Lacking Network Security Group"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "A Network Interface has a Public IP address attached but neither the NIC nor its subnet has an NSG associated."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "MEDIUM"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Network Exposure"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.network/networkinterfaces"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        has_nsg = bool(resource.properties.get("networkSecurityGroup"))
        if has_nsg:
            return findings

        # Check if NIC has a public IP
        ip_configs = resource.properties.get("ipConfigurations", [])
        has_pip = False
        pip_path = resource.json_path
        for idx, ip_conf in enumerate(ip_configs):
            props = ip_conf.get("properties", {})
            if props.get("publicIPAddress"):
                has_pip = True
                pip_path = f"{resource.json_path}.properties.ipConfigurations[{idx}].properties.publicIPAddress"
                break

        if has_pip:
            findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                rule_id=self.rule_id,
                title=self.title,
                description=f"Network interface '{resource.name}' is directly assigned a Public IP without an attached NSG.",
                severity=self.severity,
                category=self.category,
                affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                json_path=pip_path,
                evidence="publicIPAddress is defined, but networkSecurityGroup property is absent on NIC.",
                impact="Direct exposure to external traffic without host network filtering leaves the associated VM vulnerable to network scanning.",
                remediation="Associate a Network Security Group with the NIC or its containing Subnet to filter ingress traffic.",
                confidence="Direct"
            ))
        return findings
