import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from app.security_rules.base_rule import BaseSecurityRule, SecurityFinding
from app.services.template_parser import NormalizedResource

class StoragePublicBlobAccessRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-STO-001"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "Storage Account Allows Public Blob Access"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Storage accounts should disallow public / anonymous read access to containers and blobs."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "HIGH"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Storage Security"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.storage/storageaccounts"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        allow_public = resource.properties.get("allowBlobPublicAccess")
        # In ARM, boolean true or omitted in older schemas allows public blob access
        if allow_public is True or str(allow_public).lower() == "true":
            findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                rule_id=self.rule_id,
                title=self.title,
                description=f"Storage account '{resource.name}' explicitly allows public blob access.",
                severity=self.severity,
                category=self.category,
                affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                json_path=f"{resource.json_path}.properties.allowBlobPublicAccess",
                evidence="allowBlobPublicAccess is set to true.",
                impact="Anonymous internet users can read sensitive container data, logs, or backups stored in the storage account without authentication.",
                remediation="Set 'properties.allowBlobPublicAccess' to false to prevent anonymous read access."
            ))
        return findings


class StorageHttpsOnlyRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-STO-002"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "Storage Account Allows Unencrypted HTTP Traffic"

    @property

        return "Storage account allows unencrypted HTTP traffic. Secure transfer (HTTPS) must be strictly enforced."

    @property

        return "HIGH"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Storage Security"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.storage/storageaccounts"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        supports_https = resource.properties.get("supportsHttpsTrafficOnly")
        # If explicitly False, or omitted (default in legacy ARM)
        if supports_https is False or str(supports_https).lower() == "false":
            findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                rule_id=self.rule_id,
                title=self.title,
                description=f"Storage account '{resource.name}' has secure transfer disabled (HTTP allowed).",
                severity=self.severity,
                category=self.category,
                affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                json_path=f"{resource.json_path}.properties.supportsHttpsTrafficOnly",
                evidence="supportsHttpsTrafficOnly is set to false.",
                impact="Data in transit can be intercepted, sniffed, or modified via man-in-the-middle (MitM) attacks.",
                remediation="Set 'properties.supportsHttpsTrafficOnly' to true to force all API calls over TLS."
            ))
        return findings


class StorageWeakTlsRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-STO-003"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "Storage Account Minimum TLS Version Below 1.2"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Storage accounts should enforce a minimum TLS version of 1.2 to protect against legacy cryptographic weaknesses."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "MEDIUM"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Storage Security"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.storage/storageaccounts"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        min_tls = resource.properties.get("minimumTlsVersion")
        if min_tls is None or str(min_tls).upper() in ("TLS1_0", "TLS1_1"):
            evidence_msg = f"minimumTlsVersion is '{min_tls}'" if min_tls else "minimumTlsVersion property is missing (defaults to legacy TLS versions)"
            findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                rule_id=self.rule_id,
                title=self.title,
                description=f"Storage account '{resource.name}' does not enforce TLS 1.2.",
                severity=self.severity,
                category=self.category,
                affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                json_path=f"{resource.json_path}.properties.minimumTlsVersion",
                evidence=evidence_msg,
                impact="Legacy protocols (TLS 1.0/1.1) use deprecated ciphers that are vulnerable to POODLE, BEAST, and other downgrade attacks.",
                remediation="Explicitly configure 'properties.minimumTlsVersion' to 'TLS1_2'."
            ))
        return findings


class StoragePublicNetworkAccessRule(BaseSecurityRule):
    @property
    def rule_id(self) -> str:
        return "SEC-STO-004"

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return "Storage Account Public Network Access Enabled"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Storage accounts should restrict public network access and use private endpoints or virtual network firewalls where applicable."

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "LOW"

    @property
    def category(self) -> str:
        return "Storage Security"

    @property
    def applicable_resource_types(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["microsoft.storage/storageaccounts"]

    def evaluate(self, resource: NormalizedResource, all_resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        findings = []
        net_access = resource.properties.get("publicNetworkAccess")
        net_acls = resource.properties.get("networkAcls", {})
        default_action = net_acls.get("defaultAction")

        if (net_access and str(net_access).lower() == "enabled") or (default_action and str(default_action).lower() == "allow"):
            findings.append(SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                rule_id=self.rule_id,
                title=self.title,
                description=f"Storage account '{resource.name}' is reachable from all public networks.",
                severity=self.severity,
                category=self.category,
                affected_resource_id=resource.id,
                affected_resource_name=resource.name,
                affected_resource_type=resource.type,
                json_path=f"{resource.json_path}.properties.publicNetworkAccess",
                evidence=f"publicNetworkAccess: '{net_access}', networkAcls.defaultAction: '{default_action}'",
                impact="Broad network accessibility increases attack surface to authentication brute-force or endpoint DDoS attacks.",
                remediation="Restrict access by specifying virtual network rules, private endpoints, or set defaultAction to Deny.",
                confidence="Incomplete Evidence"
            ))
        return findings

"""Deterministic security rule definitions for Azure ARM templates."""



================================================================================
FILE PATH: app\services\dependency_resolver.py
================================================================================
import re
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple, Optional, Set
from app.services.template_parser import NormalizedResource

class DependencyResolver:
    """
    Analyzes normalized ARM resources to discover deployment dependencies,
    network topologies, security boundaries, data flow channels, and authorization relationships.
    """

    def __init__(self, resources: List[NormalizedResource]):
        self.resources = resources
        self.resource_by_id: Dict[str, NormalizedResource] = {r.id.lower(): r for r in resources}
        self.resource_by_name: Dict[str, NormalizedResource] = {r.name.lower(): r for r in resources}
        
        # Also index parent prefix names and tokens for child resources (e.g. "parent" for "parent/child")
        self.resource_by_short_name: Dict[str, NormalizedResource] = {}
        for r in resources:
            parts = r.name.lower().split("/")
            for part in parts:
                part_clean = part.strip()
                if part_clean and part_clean not in self.resource_by_short_name:
                    self.resource_by_short_name[part_clean] = r
            # Also register type-shortname combo
            short_type = r.type.split("/")[-1].lower()
            self.resource_by_short_name[f"{short_type}:{parts[-1]}"] = r

    def resolve_edges(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Builds graph edges with categorized relationship types and traffic flow direction.
        Returns a list of edge dictionaries formatted for Cytoscape.js and Native SVG.
        """
        edges: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        edge_set: Set[Tuple[str, str, str]] = set()

        def add_edge(source_id: str, target_id: str, relation_type: str, label: str):
            if not source_id or not target_id or source_id == target_id:
                return
            edge_key = (source_id, target_id, relation_type)
            if edge_key not in edge_set:
                edge_set.add(edge_key)

                # Determine traffic flow direction:
                # - "incoming": External traffic entering from Public IP / Gateway
                # - "outgoing": Compute workload traffic sending requests to Storage, Cache, DB, Secrets
                # - "control": Policy or Authorization boundary
                # - "forward": General network association or dependency
                traffic_flow = "forward"
                if relation_type == "exposure":
                    traffic_flow = "incoming"
                elif relation_type in ("data_flow", "integration"):
                    traffic_flow = "outgoing"
                elif relation_type in ("security_boundary", "authorization"):
                    traffic_flow = "control"

                edges.append({
                    "data": {
                        "id": f"edge_{len(edges) + 1}",
                        "source": source_id,
                        "target": target_id,
                        "relation_type": relation_type,
                        "label": label,
                        "traffic_flow": traffic_flow
                    }
                })

        for res in self.resources:
            res_type_lower = res.type.lower()

            # 1. Explicit dependsOn edges with intelligent relationship typing
            for dep in res.depends_on:
                target_id = self._match_resource_id(dep)
                if target_id and target_id != res.id:
                    target_res = self.resource_by_id.get(target_id.lower())
                    rel_type = "dependency"
                    rel_label = "depends on"
                    if target_res:
                        t_cat = target_res.category.lower()
                        t_type = target_res.type.lower()
                        if "network security" in t_cat or "networksecuritygroup" in t_type:
                            rel_type = "security_boundary"
                            rel_label = "protected by NSG"
                        elif "exposure" in t_cat or "publicip" in t_type:
                            rel_type = "exposure"
                            rel_label = "assigned Public IP"
                        elif "storage" in t_cat or "storage" in t_type:
                            rel_type = "data_flow"
                            rel_label = "stores data in Storage"
                        elif "redis" in t_cat or "redis" in t_type:
                            rel_type = "data_flow"
                            rel_label = "caches in Redis"
                        elif "database" in t_cat or "sql" in t_type:
                            rel_type = "data_flow"
                            rel_label = "queries Database"
                        elif "keyvault" in t_cat or "keyvault" in t_type:
                            rel_type = "integration"
                            rel_label = "secures secrets in Key Vault"
                        elif "identity" in t_cat or "role" in t_type:
                            rel_type = "authorization"
                            rel_label = "assigns custom role"
                        elif "network" in t_cat:
                            rel_type = "network_association"
                            rel_label = "network link to"
                    add_edge(res.id, target_id, rel_type, rel_label)

            # 2. Virtual Machine network interfaces
            if res_type_lower == "microsoft.compute/virtualmachines":
                nic_refs = res.properties.get("networkProfile", {}).get("networkInterfaces", [])
                for nic_ref in nic_refs:
                    target_nic = self._match_resource_id(nic_ref.get("id", ""))
                    if target_nic:
                        add_edge(res.id, target_nic, "network_association", "uses NIC")

            # 3. Network Interface Card (NIC) associations
            if res_type_lower == "microsoft.network/networkinterfaces":
                # Check NSG association on NIC
                nsg_ref = res.properties.get("networkSecurityGroup", {}).get("id", "")
                target_nsg = self._match_resource_id(nsg_ref)
                if target_nsg:
                    add_edge(res.id, target_nsg, "security_boundary", "protected by NSG")

                # Check Subnet & Public IP associations on IP configurations
                ip_configs = res.properties.get("ipConfigurations", [])
                for ip_conf in ip_configs:
                    ip_props = ip_conf.get("properties", {})
                    # Subnet link
                    subnet_ref = ip_props.get("subnet", {}).get("id", "")
                    target_subnet = self._match_resource_id(subnet_ref)
                    if target_subnet:
                        add_edge(res.id, target_subnet, "network_association", "attached to subnet")

                    # Public IP link
                    pip_ref = ip_props.get("publicIPAddress", {}).get("id", "")
                    target_pip = self._match_resource_id(pip_ref)
                    if target_pip:
                        add_edge(res.id, target_pip, "exposure", "assigned Public IP")

            # 4. Subnet associations
            if res_type_lower in ("microsoft.network/virtualnetworks/subnets", "microsoft.network/subnets"):
                nsg_ref = res.properties.get("networkSecurityGroup", {}).get("id", "")
                target_nsg = self._match_resource_id(nsg_ref)
                if target_nsg:
                    add_edge(res.id, target_nsg, "security_boundary", "protected by NSG")

                for other in self.resources:
                    if other.type.lower() == "microsoft.network/virtualnetworks":
                        if other.name.lower() in res.id.lower() or other.id.lower() in res.id.lower():
                            add_edge(res.id, other.id, "containment", "subnet of")
                            break

            # 5. Role assignments -> Role Definitions and Scopes
            if res_type_lower == "microsoft.authorization/roleassignments":
                # Match custom or assigned role definition in template
                role_def_ref = res.properties.get("roleDefinitionId", "")
                target_role = self._match_resource_id(role_def_ref)
                if target_role:
                    add_edge(res.id, target_role, "authorization", "assigns role")
                else:
                    # Check if any roleDefinition matches by name or id in template
                    for other in self.resources:
                        if other.type.lower() == "microsoft.authorization/roledefinitions":
                            if other.name.lower() in str(role_def_ref).lower() or other.id.lower() in str(role_def_ref).lower():
                                add_edge(res.id, other.id, "authorization", "assigns role")
                                break

                # Match scope
                scope_ref = res.properties.get("scope", "")
                target_scope = self._match_resource_id(scope_ref)
                if target_scope:
                    add_edge(res.id, target_scope, "authorization", "scoped to")

                # Match principal
                principal_ref = res.properties.get("principalId", "")
                target_principal = self._match_resource_id(principal_ref)
                if target_principal:
                    add_edge(res.id, target_principal, "authorization", "granted to")

            # 6. App Service web app hosting, networking, appSettings, and connectionStrings
            if res_type_lower in ("microsoft.web/sites", "microsoft.web/sites/slots"):
                # App Service Plan link
                server_farm_id = res.properties.get("serverFarmId")
                target_plan = self._match_resource_id(server_farm_id)
                if target_plan:
                    add_edge(res.id, target_plan, "hosting", "hosted on Plan")

                # VNet integration
                vnet_subnet_id = res.properties.get("virtualNetworkSubnetId")
                target_vnet_subnet = self._match_resource_id(vnet_subnet_id)
                if target_vnet_subnet:
                    add_edge(res.id, target_vnet_subnet, "network_association", "VNet integrated")

                # AppSettings references (Storage, Redis, Key Vault, Database, etc.)
                site_config = res.properties.get("siteConfig", {})
                app_settings = site_config.get("appSettings", [])
                self._extract_app_service_links(res, app_settings, add_edge)

                # ConnectionStrings references (SQL, Storage, etc.)
                connection_strings = site_config.get("connectionStrings", [])
                self._extract_app_service_links(res, connection_strings, add_edge)

            # 7. SQL Database parent SQL Server or transparent data encryption
            if "microsoft.sql/servers" in res_type_lower:
                if "/" in res.name:
                    parent_server_name = res.name.split("/")[0].lower()
                    if parent_server_name in self.resource_by_name:
                        add_edge(res.id, self.resource_by_name[parent_server_name].id, "containment", "hosted on SQL Server")
                for other in self.resources:
                    if other.type.lower() == "microsoft.sql/servers" and other.id != res.id:
                        if other.name.lower() in res.name.lower():
                            add_edge(res.id, other.id, "containment", "hosted on SQL Server")
                            break

            # 8. Diagnostic settings log destinations
            if res_type_lower == "microsoft.insights/diagnosticsettings":
                storage_id = res.properties.get("storageAccountId")
                target_sto = self._match_resource_id(storage_id)
                if target_sto:
                    add_edge(res.id, target_sto, "monitoring", "logs to Storage")

                workspace_id = res.properties.get("workspaceId")
                target_ws = self._match_resource_id(workspace_id)
                if target_ws:
                    add_edge(res.id, target_ws, "monitoring", "logs to Workspace")

            # 9. Private Endpoints
            if res_type_lower == "microsoft.network/privateendpoints":
                sub_id = res.properties.get("subnet", {}).get("id")
                target_sub = self._match_resource_id(sub_id)
                if target_sub:
                    add_edge(res.id, target_sub, "network_association", "connected to Subnet")

                connections = res.properties.get("privateLinkServiceConnections", [])
                for conn in connections:
                    target_res_id = conn.get("properties", {}).get("privateLinkServiceId")
                    target_svc = self._match_resource_id(target_res_id)
                    if target_svc:
                        add_edge(res.id, target_svc, "network_association", "private link to")

            # 10. General Cross-Resource Property Reference Discovery
            # Recursively inspect all properties of the resource for references to other resources
            self._discover_deep_references(res, res.properties, add_edge)

        return edges

    def _extract_app_service_links(self, source_res: NormalizedResource, settings_data: Any, add_edge):
        """Discovers data-flow and integration edges from App Service config entries."""
        if not settings_data:
            return

        entries = []
        if isinstance(settings_data, list):
            for item in settings_data:
                if isinstance(item, dict):
                    entries.append((item.get("name", ""), item.get("value", "") or item.get("connectionString", "")))
        elif isinstance(settings_data, dict):
            for k, v in settings_data.items():
                entries.append((k, v if isinstance(v, str) else str(v)))

        for key, val in entries:
            target_id = self._match_resource_id(val) or self._match_resource_id(key)
            if target_id and target_id != source_res.id:
                target_res = self.resource_by_id.get(target_id.lower())
                if target_res:
                    cat = target_res.category.lower()
                    t_type = target_res.type.lower()
                    if "storage" in cat or "storage" in t_type:
                        add_edge(source_res.id, target_id, "data_flow", "connects to Storage")
                    elif "redis" in cat or "redis" in t_type:
                        add_edge(source_res.id, target_id, "data_flow", "caches in Redis")
                    elif "keyvault" in cat or "keyvault" in t_type:
                        add_edge(source_res.id, target_id, "integration", "references Key Vault")
                    elif "database" in cat or "sql" in t_type:
                        add_edge(source_res.id, target_id, "data_flow", "queries SQL Database")
                    elif "network" in cat:
                        add_edge(source_res.id, target_id, "network_association", "connected to Network")
                    else:
                        add_edge(source_res.id, target_id, "data_flow", f"connects to {target_res.category}")

    def _discover_deep_references(self, source_res: NormalizedResource, data: Any, add_edge):
        """Recursively scans complex property structures for references to other resources in the template."""
        if isinstance(data, dict):
            for k, v in data.items():
                self._discover_deep_references(source_res, v, add_edge)
        elif isinstance(data, list):
            for item in data:
                self._discover_deep_references(source_res, item, add_edge)
        elif isinstance(data, str) and len(data) > 3:
            # Skip short generic values
            if data.lower() in ("true", "false", "enabled", "disabled", "eastus", "westus", "standard_lrs"):
                return
            target_id = self._match_resource_id(data)
            if target_id and target_id != source_res.id:
                target_res = self.resource_by_id.get(target_id.lower())
                if target_res:
                    cat = target_res.category.lower()
                    if "storage" in cat or "database" in cat or "redis" in cat:
                        add_edge(source_res.id, target_id, "data_flow", f"data flow to {target_res.name}")
                    elif "keyvault" in cat:
                        add_edge(source_res.id, target_id, "integration", f"secures secrets in {target_res.name}")
                    elif "network" in cat:
                        add_edge(source_res.id, target_id, "network_association", f"network link to {target_res.name}")
                    else:
                        add_edge(source_res.id, target_id, "dependency", f"references {target_res.name}")

    def _match_resource_id(self, reference: Any) -> str:
        """Attempts to match a reference string, ARM expression, or resourceId to an existing resource ID."""
        if not reference or not isinstance(reference, str):
            return ""

        ref_str = reference.strip()
        ref_lower = ref_str.lower()

        # 1. Direct match in known IDs
        if ref_lower in self.resource_by_id:
            return self.resource_by_id[ref_lower].id

        # 2. Direct match by exact resource name
        if ref_lower in self.resource_by_name:
            return self.resource_by_name[ref_lower].id

        # 3. Direct match by short name
        if ref_lower in self.resource_by_short_name:
            return self.resource_by_short_name[ref_lower].id

        # 4. Extract resourceId arguments: supports resourceId('type', 'name') and [resourceId: 'type', 'name']
        resource_id_matches = re.findall(r"resourceId[\s:(]+[^)\]]*['\"]([^'\"]+)['\"]", ref_str, re.IGNORECASE)
        # Also collect all quoted tokens inside the reference
        quoted_tokens = re.findall(r"['\"]([^'\"]+)['\"]", ref_str)
        candidates = list(reversed(resource_id_matches)) + list(reversed(quoted_tokens))
        for cand in candidates:
            cand_lower = cand.strip().lower()
            if cand_lower in self.resource_by_name:
                return self.resource_by_name[cand_lower].id
            if cand_lower in self.resource_by_short_name:
                return self.resource_by_short_name[cand_lower].id

        # 5. Try path split by '/'
        # Handle cases like /subscriptions/.../providers/Microsoft.X/resName
        cleaned = re.sub(r"[\[\]'\"\)\(]", "", ref_str).strip()
        parts = cleaned.split("/")
        if parts:
            last_part = parts[-1].strip().lower()
            if last_part in self.resource_by_name:
                return self.resource_by_name[last_part].id
            if last_part in self.resource_by_short_name:
                return self.resource_by_short_name[last_part].id

        # 6. Check if any known resource name is present as a standalone token or substring
        # Prioritize longest matching resource name
        matching_resources = []
        for r_name, res in self.resource_by_name.items():
            if len(r_name) < 4:
                continue
            # Token boundary or delimiters
            pattern = r"(?:\b|_|-|/|'|\"|=)" + re.escape(r_name) + r"(?:\b|_|-|/|'|\"|;|\.|\Z)"
            if re.search(pattern, ref_lower):
                matching_resources.append((len(r_name), res.id))
            elif r_name in ref_lower and (res.type.lower() in ref_lower or "/" in res.name):
                matching_resources.append((len(r_name), res.id))

        if matching_resources:
            matching_resources.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
            return matching_resources[0][1]

        return ""

FILE PATH: app\services\report_generator.py

import datetime
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from app.services.template_parser import NormalizedResource
from app.security_rules.base_rule import SecurityFinding

SEVERITY_ORDER = ["CRITICAL", "HIGH", "MEDIUM", "LOW", "INFORMATIONAL"]

class ReportGenerator:
    """Aggregates security scan results, calculates security metrics, and prepares audit reports."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        file_id: str,
        filename: str,
        resources: List[NormalizedResource],
        edges: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        findings: List[SecurityFinding],
        metadata: Dict[str, Any],
        warnings: List[str]
    ):
        self.file_id = file_id
        self.filename = filename
        self.resources = resources
        self.edges = edges
        self.findings = findings
        self.metadata = metadata
        self.warnings = warnings

    def generate_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Calculates executive summary metrics for dashboard cards."""
        severity_counts = {s: 0 for s in SEVERITY_ORDER}
        for f in self.findings:
            sev = f.severity.upper()
            if sev in severity_counts:
                severity_counts[sev] += 1
            else:
                severity_counts["INFORMATIONAL"] += 1

        # Count findings per resource
        resource_finding_counts: Dict[str, int] = {}
        for f in self.findings:
            r_name = f.affected_resource_name
            resource_finding_counts[r_name] = resource_finding_counts.get(r_name, 0) + 1

        # Top affected resources
        top_affected = sorted(
            [{"name": k, "count": v} for k, v in resource_finding_counts.items()],
            key=lambda x: x["count"],
            reverse=True
        )[:5]

        # Categories affected
        categories_affected: Dict[str, int] = {}
        for f in self.findings:
            categories_affected[f.category] = categories_affected.get(f.category, 0) + 1

        # Unique rule calculation
        unique_rule_ids = sorted(list({f.rule_id for f in self.findings}))

        return {
            "total_resources": len(self.resources),
            "total_relationships": len(self.edges),
            "total_findings": len(self.findings),
            "unique_rules_count": len(unique_rule_ids),
            "unique_rule_ids": unique_rule_ids,
            "severity_counts": severity_counts,
            "top_affected_resources": top_affected,
            "categories_affected": categories_affected,
            "template_metadata": self.metadata,
            "warnings_count": len(self.warnings),
            "warnings": self.warnings
        }

    def generate_export_report(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Produces a comprehensive JSON export of the security audit."""
        summary = self.generate_summary()
        return {
            "report_metadata": {
                "report_version": "1.0.0",
                "tool_name": "Azure Security Analyzer",
                "scan_timestamp": datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z"),
                "scanned_file": self.filename,
                "file_id": self.file_id
            },
            "summary": summary,
            "findings": [f.to_dict() for f in self.findings],
            "resources": [
                {
                    "id": r.id,
                    "name": r.name,
                    "type": r.type,
                    "category": r.category,
                    "location": r.location,
                    "depends_on": r.depends_on,
                    "json_path": r.json_path
                }
                for r in self.resources
            ],
            "limitations_and_warnings": self.warnings
        }

from typing import List, Dict, Any
from app.services.template_parser import NormalizedResource
from app.services.dependency_resolver import DependencyResolver
from app.utils.sanitization import mask_sensitive_data

SEVERITY_WEIGHT = {
    "CRITICAL": 5,
    "HIGH": 4,
    "MEDIUM": 3,
    "LOW": 2,
    "INFORMATIONAL": 1,
    "CLEAN": 0
}

def get_short_type(res_type: str) -> str:
    """Returns an intuitive friendly name for Azure resource types."""
    mapping = {
        "microsoft.compute/virtualmachines": "Virtual Machine",
        "microsoft.compute/virtualmachines/extensions": "VM Extension",
        "microsoft.compute/availabilitysets": "Availability Set",
        "microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets": "VM Scale Set",
        "microsoft.network/virtualnetworks": "Virtual Network",
        "microsoft.network/virtualnetworks/subnets": "Subnet",
        "microsoft.network/subnets": "Subnet",
        "microsoft.network/networksecuritygroups": "Network Security Group",
        "microsoft.network/networkinterfaces": "Network Interface",
        "microsoft.network/publicipaddresses": "Public IP",
        "microsoft.network/loadbalancers": "Load Balancer",
        "microsoft.network/applicationgateways": "Application Gateway",
        "microsoft.network/privateendpoints": "Private Endpoint",
        "microsoft.storage/storageaccounts": "Storage Account",
        "microsoft.keyvault/vaults": "Key Vault",
        "microsoft.web/sites": "App Service",
        "microsoft.web/serverfarms": "App Service Plan",
        "microsoft.sql/servers": "SQL Server",
        "microsoft.sql/servers/databases": "SQL Database",
        "microsoft.authorization/roleassignments": "Role Assignment",
        "microsoft.authorization/roledefinitions": "Role Definition",
        "microsoft.managedidentity/userassignedidentities": "Managed Identity",
        "microsoft.automation/automationaccounts": "Automation Account",
        "microsoft.containerservice/managedclusters": "Kubernetes Cluster",
        "microsoft.logic/workflows": "Logic App",
        "microsoft.cache/redis": "Redis Cache",
        "microsoft.insights/diagnosticsettings": "Diagnostic Settings",
    }
    low = (res_type or "").lower()
    if low in mapping:
        return mapping[low]
    parts = res_type.split("/")
    return parts[-1] if parts else "Azure Resource"

def determine_resource_risk(findings_for_res: List[Any]) -> str:
    """Determine highest risk level for a resource based on its findings."""
    if not findings_for_res:
        return "CLEAN"
    highest_sev = "CLEAN"
    max_weight = 0
    for finding in findings_for_res:
        sev = finding.severity.upper() if hasattr(finding, 'severity') else finding.get('severity', 'CLEAN').upper()
        weight = SEVERITY_WEIGHT.get(sev, 0)
        if weight > max_weight:
            max_weight = weight
            highest_sev = sev
    return highest_sev

class ResourceMapper:
    """Transforms normalized resources and security findings into Cytoscape.js graph structure."""

    def __init__(self, resources: List[NormalizedResource], findings: List[Any]):
        self.resources = resources
        self.findings = findings
        # Index findings by affected_resource_id
        self.findings_by_resource: Dict[str, List[Any]] = {}
        for f in findings:
            r_id = f.affected_resource_id if hasattr(f, 'affected_resource_id') else f.get('affected_resource_id')
            if r_id:
                self.findings_by_resource.setdefault(r_id.lower(), []).append(f)

    def generate_graph(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Generate Cytoscape JSON payload containing nodes and edges."""
        nodes = []
        for res in self.resources:
            res_findings = self.findings_by_resource.get(res.id.lower(), [])
            risk_level = determine_resource_risk(res_findings)

            safe_properties = mask_sensitive_data(res.properties)

            node_data = {
                "id": res.id,
                "label": res.name,
                "type": res.type,
                "type_short": get_short_type(res.type),
                "category": res.category,
                "location": res.location,
                "api_version": res.api_version,
                "risk_level": risk_level,
                "finding_count": len(res_findings),
                "findings": [
                    {
                        "finding_id": f.finding_id if hasattr(f, 'finding_id') else f.get('finding_id'),
                        "rule_id": f.rule_id if hasattr(f, 'rule_id') else f.get('rule_id'),
                        "title": f.title if hasattr(f, 'title') else f.get('title'),
                        "severity": f.severity if hasattr(f, 'severity') else f.get('severity')
                    }
                    for f in res_findings
                ],
                "json_path": res.json_path,
                "properties": safe_properties
            }
            nodes.append({"data": node_data})

        # Resolve edges
        resolver = DependencyResolver(self.resources)
        edges = resolver.resolve_edges()

        return {
            "nodes": nodes,
            "edges": edges,
            "stats": {
                "total_nodes": len(nodes),
                "total_edges": len(edges)
            }
        }

FILE PATH: app\services\security_scanner.py

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Type
from app.services.template_parser import NormalizedResource
from app.security_rules.base_rule import BaseSecurityRule, SecurityFinding
from app.security_rules.network_rules import (
    NSGOpenSSHRule,
    NSGOpenRDPRule,
    NSGAllowAllInboundRule,
    UnprotectedPublicIPRule,
    NSGOpenHTTPRule
)
from app.security_rules.storage_rules import (
    StoragePublicBlobAccessRule,
    StorageHttpsOnlyRule,
    StorageWeakTlsRule,
    StoragePublicNetworkAccessRule
)
from app.security_rules.identity_rules import (
    BroadSubscriptionRoleAssignmentRule,
    CustomRoleWildcardRule
)
from app.security_rules.app_service_rules import (
    AppServiceRemoteDebuggingRule,
    AppServiceFtpsEnforcementRule,
    AppServiceMinTlsVersionRule,
    AppServiceCorsWildcardRule,
    AppServiceManagedIdentityRule
)
from app.security_rules.keyvault_rules import (
    KeyVaultPurgeProtectionRule
)
from app.security_rules.encryption_rules import (
    AppServiceHttpsOnlyRule,
    SqlDatabaseTdeRule,
    StorageInfrastructureEncryptionRule,
    AutomationVariableEncryptionRule,
    RedisNonSslPortRule
)
from app.security_rules.logging_rules import (
    MissingDiagnosticSettingsRule
)

DEFAULT_RULES: List[Type[BaseSecurityRule]] = [
    # Network security rules (5 rules)
    NSGOpenSSHRule,
    NSGOpenRDPRule,
    NSGAllowAllInboundRule,
    UnprotectedPublicIPRule,
    NSGOpenHTTPRule,
    # Storage security rules (4 rules)
    StoragePublicBlobAccessRule,
    StorageHttpsOnlyRule,
    StorageWeakTlsRule,
    StoragePublicNetworkAccessRule,
    # App Service rules (5 rules)
    AppServiceRemoteDebuggingRule,
    AppServiceFtpsEnforcementRule,
    AppServiceMinTlsVersionRule,
    AppServiceCorsWildcardRule,
    AppServiceManagedIdentityRule,
    # Key Vault rules (1 rule)
    KeyVaultPurgeProtectionRule,
    # Identity & Authorization rules (2 rules)
    BroadSubscriptionRoleAssignmentRule,
    CustomRoleWildcardRule,
    # Encryption & Transit rules (5 rules)
    AppServiceHttpsOnlyRule,
    SqlDatabaseTdeRule,
    StorageInfrastructureEncryptionRule,
    AutomationVariableEncryptionRule,
    RedisNonSslPortRule,
    # Logging & Monitoring rules (1 rule)
    MissingDiagnosticSettingsRule
]

class SecurityScanner:
    """Central engine that coordinates the execution of deterministic Azure security rules."""

    def __init__(self, rule_classes: List[Type[BaseSecurityRule]] = None):
        classes = rule_classes or DEFAULT_RULES
        self.rules: List[BaseSecurityRule] = [cls() for cls in classes]

    def scan(self, resources: List[NormalizedResource], template_metadata: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
        """
        Runs all registered security rules against the resources.
        Returns a flat list of SecurityFinding objects.
        """
        all_findings: List[SecurityFinding] = []

        for resource in resources:
            res_type_lower = resource.type.lower()
            for rule in self.rules:
                applicable_types = [t.lower() for t in rule.applicable_resource_types]
                if any(res_type_lower == app_t or res_type_lower.endswith("/" + app_t) for app_t in applicable_types):
                    rule_findings = rule.evaluate(resource, resources, template_metadata)
                    all_findings.extend(rule_findings)

        return all_findings

FILE PATH: app\services\template_parser.py

import re
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

RESOURCE_CATEGORIES = {
    "Microsoft.Compute/virtualMachines": "Compute",
    "Microsoft.Compute/virtualMachineScaleSets": "Compute",
    "Microsoft.Compute/disks": "Compute Storage",
    "Microsoft.Network/virtualNetworks": "Network",
    "Microsoft.Network/virtualNetworks/subnets": "Network",
    "Microsoft.Network/subnets": "Network",
    "Microsoft.Network/networkInterfaces": "Network",
    "Microsoft.Network/networkSecurityGroups": "Network Security",
    "Microsoft.Network/publicIPAddresses": "Network Exposure",
    "Microsoft.Network/loadBalancers": "Network",
    "Microsoft.Network/applicationGateways": "Network Security",
    "Microsoft.Storage/storageAccounts": "Storage",
    "Microsoft.KeyVault/vaults": "Security & Identity",
    "Microsoft.Authorization/roleAssignments": "Identity & Access",
    "Microsoft.Authorization/roleDefinitions": "Identity & Access",
    "Microsoft.ManagedIdentity/userAssignedIdentities": "Identity & Access",
    "Microsoft.Web/serverfarms": "Compute",
    "Microsoft.Web/sites": "App Service",
    "Microsoft.Sql/servers": "Database",
    "Microsoft.Sql/servers/databases": "Database",
    "Microsoft.Insights/diagnosticSettings": "Logging & Monitoring",
}

def get_category_for_type(res_type: str) -> str:
    """Categorize an Azure resource type."""
    if not res_type:
        return "General"
    for prefix, cat in RESOURCE_CATEGORIES.items():
        if res_type.lower() == prefix.lower():
            return cat
    # Prefix-based fallbacks
    lower_type = res_type.lower()
    if "microsoft.compute" in lower_type:
        return "Compute"
    if "microsoft.network" in lower_type:
        return "Network"
    if "microsoft.storage" in lower_type:
        return "Storage"
    if "microsoft.keyvault" in lower_type:
        return "Security & Identity"
    if "microsoft.authorization" in lower_type:
        return "Identity & Access"
    if "microsoft.web" in lower_type:
        return "App Service"
    if "microsoft.sql" in lower_type:
        return "Database"
    if "microsoft.insights" in lower_type or "microsoft.operationalinsights" in lower_type:
        return "Logging & Monitoring"
    return "Other Services"

@dataclass
class NormalizedResource:
    id: str
    name: str
    type: str
    api_version: str
    location: str
    properties: Dict[str, Any]
    depends_on: List[str]
    category: str
    json_path: str
    raw_json: Dict[str, Any]
    tags: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    unresolved_expressions: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)

class SafeArmExpressionEvaluator:
    """
    Safely resolves common ARM template expressions without python eval().
    Supports: parameters(), variables(), concat(), format(), resourceId(), if(), equals()
    Gracefully flags unsupported or runtime functions as symbolic values.
    """
    def __init__(self, parameters: Dict[str, Any], variables: Dict[str, Any]):
        self.parameters = parameters or {}
        self.variables = variables or {}
        self.resolved_variables: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._resolve_variables()

    def _resolve_variables(self):
        """Pre-evaluate variable expressions if they reference parameters or other variables."""
        for k, v in self.variables.items():
            if isinstance(v, str) and v.startswith("[") and v.endswith("]"):
                self.resolved_variables[k] = self.evaluate(v)
            else:
                self.resolved_variables[k] = v

    def evaluate(self, expr_str: Any) -> Any:
        """Evaluate an ARM expression string if enclosed in '[ ... ]'."""
        if not isinstance(expr_str, str):
            return expr_str
        
        trimmed = expr_str.strip()
        # ARM escape: '[[text]' represents literal '[text]'
        if trimmed.startswith("[[") and trimmed.endswith("]"):
            return trimmed[1:]
        
        if not (trimmed.startswith("[") and trimmed.endswith("]")):
            return expr_str
        
        inner = trimmed[1:-1].strip()
        try:
            return self._eval_inner(inner)
        except Exception:
            # Fall back safely to symbolic representation
            return f"[Unresolved: {inner}]"

    def _eval_inner(self, inner: str) -> Any:
        inner = inner.strip()
        
        func_match = re.match(r'^([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\((.*)\)$', inner, re.DOTALL)
        if not func_match:
            # Check if property navigation or unhandled function call pattern (e.g. func(...).prop)
            if "(" in inner and ")" in inner:
                return f"[Symbolic:{inner}]"
            # Check if literal boolean, number, or quoted string
            if inner.lower() == 'true':
                return True
            if inner.lower() == 'false':
                return False
            if (inner.startswith("'") and inner.endswith("'")) or (inner.startswith('"') and inner.endswith('"')):
                return inner[1:-1]
            try:
                if '.' in inner:
                    return float(inner)
                return int(inner)
            except ValueError:
                if any(c in inner for c in ('.', '[', ']', '(', ')')):
                    return f"[Symbolic:{inner}]"
                return inner

        func_name = func_match.group(1).lower()
        raw_args_str = func_match.group(2).strip()
        args = self._split_arguments(raw_args_str)

        if func_name == "parameters":
            if args:
                pname = self._eval_inner(args[0])
                param_def = self.parameters.get(pname, {})
                if isinstance(param_def, dict) and "defaultValue" in param_def:
                    return param_def["defaultValue"]
                elif isinstance(param_def, (str, int, float, bool)):
                    return param_def
                return f"[Param:{pname}]"
            return "[Param:unknown]"

        elif func_name == "variables":
            if args:
                vname = self._eval_inner(args[0])
                if vname in self.resolved_variables:
                    return self.resolved_variables[vname]
                if vname in self.variables:
                    val = self.variables[vname]
                    if isinstance(val, str) and val.startswith("["):
                        return self.evaluate(val)
                    return val
                return f"[Var:{vname}]"
            return "[Var:unknown]"

        elif func_name == "concat":
            evaluated_args = [str(self._eval_inner(arg)) for arg in args]
            return "".join(evaluated_args)

        elif func_name == "format":
            if args:
                fmt_str = str(self._eval_inner(args[0]))
                format_args = [str(self._eval_inner(arg)) for arg in args[1:]]
                for idx, val in enumerate(format_args):
                    fmt_str = fmt_str.replace(f"{{{idx}}}", val)
                return fmt_str
            return ""

        elif func_name == "equals":
            if len(args) == 2:
                v1 = self._eval_inner(args[0])
                v2 = self._eval_inner(args[1])
                return str(v1).lower() == str(v2).lower()
            return False

        elif func_name == "if":
            if len(args) == 3:
                cond = self._eval_inner(args[0])
                is_true = cond is True or str(cond).lower() == 'true'
                return self._eval_inner(args[1]) if is_true else self._eval_inner(args[2])
            return ""

        elif func_name == "resourceid":
            evaluated_args = [str(self._eval_inner(arg)) for arg in args]
            # e.g., resourceId('Microsoft.Network/virtualNetworks', 'vnetName')
            # normalized representation: providers/type/name
            if len(evaluated_args) >= 2:
                res_type = evaluated_args[0]
                res_names = evaluated_args[1:]
                return f"providers/{res_type}/{'/'.join(res_names)}"
            elif len(evaluated_args) == 1:
                return f"providers/{evaluated_args[0]}"
            return f"[resourceId:{raw_args_str}]"

        elif func_name in ("subscriptionresourceid", "tenantresourceid"):
            evaluated_args = [str(self._eval_inner(arg)) for arg in args]
            return f"providers/{'/'.join(evaluated_args)}"

        # Runtime or complex expressions
        return f"[Symbolic:{func_name}({raw_args_str})]"

    def _split_arguments(self, raw_args: str) -> List[str]:
        """Safely split comma-separated function arguments honoring nested parentheses and quotes."""
        args = []
        current = []
        depth = 0
        in_quote = False
        quote_char = ''

        for char in raw_args:
            if char in ("'", '"') and not in_quote:
                in_quote = True
                quote_char = char
                current.append(char)
            elif char == quote_char and in_quote:
                in_quote = False
                quote_char = ''
                current.append(char)
            elif in_quote:
                current.append(char)
            elif char == '(':
                depth += 1
                current.append(char)
            elif char == ')':
                depth -= 1
                current.append(char)
            elif char == ',' and depth == 0:
                args.append("".join(current).strip())
                current = []
            else:
                current.append(char)

        if current:
            token = "".join(current).strip()
            if token:
                args.append(token)
        return args


class ArmTemplateParser:
    """Parses ARM JSON templates into normalized internal resource representations."""

    def __init__(self, template_data: Dict[str, Any]):
        self.raw_data = template_data
        self.parameters = template_data.get("parameters", {})
        self.variables = template_data.get("variables", {})
        self.evaluator = SafeArmExpressionEvaluator(self.parameters, self.variables)
        self.warnings: List[str] = []
        self.unresolved_constructs: List[str] = []

    def parse(self) -> Tuple[List[NormalizedResource], Dict[str, Any], List[str]]:
        """
        Parses and returns:
            - normalized_resources: list of NormalizedResource
            - metadata: dictionary containing schema, contentVersion, parameter/variable counts
            - warnings: any warnings or unsupported constructs encountered
        """
        normalized_resources: List[NormalizedResource] = []
        raw_resources = self.raw_data.get("resources", [])

        metadata = {
            "schema": self.raw_data.get("$schema", "Unknown"),
            "contentVersion": self.raw_data.get("contentVersion", "1.0.0.0"),
            "parameter_count": len(self.parameters),
            "variable_count": len(self.variables),
            "total_raw_resources": len(raw_resources)
        }

        for idx, res in enumerate(raw_resources):
            parsed_res = self._process_resource(res, f"$.resources[{idx}]")
            if parsed_res:
                normalized_resources.append(parsed_res)
                
                # Check for nested sub-resources (e.g. subnets inside virtualNetworks)
                sub_resources = self._extract_child_resources(res, parsed_res, f"$.resources[{idx}]")
                normalized_resources.extend(sub_resources)

        return normalized_resources, metadata, self.warnings

    def _process_resource(self, res: Dict[str, Any], json_path: str, parent: Optional[NormalizedResource] = None) -> Optional[NormalizedResource]:
        res_type = res.get("type", "")
        raw_name = res.get("name", f"Unnamed_Resource_{json_path}")
        
        # Evaluate expressions in name, type, location
        resolved_name = str(self._resolve_value(raw_name))
        resolved_type = str(self._resolve_value(res_type))
        location = str(self._resolve_value(res.get("location", "global")))
        api_version = str(res.get("apiVersion", ""))
        
        # Resolve properties
        raw_properties = res.get("properties", {})
        resolved_properties = self._resolve_deep(raw_properties) if isinstance(raw_properties, dict) else {}

        # Resolve dependsOn
        raw_depends = res.get("dependsOn", [])
        resolved_depends = []
        if isinstance(raw_depends, list):
            for dep in raw_depends:
                resolved_dep = str(self._resolve_value(dep))
                resolved_depends.append(resolved_dep)

        # Generate a stable resource id
        if parent:
            synthetic_id = f"{parent.id}/{resolved_name}"
        else:
            synthetic_id = f"providers/{resolved_type}/{resolved_name}"

        category = get_category_for_type(resolved_type)
        tags = res.get("tags", {})

        return NormalizedResource(
            id=synthetic_id,
            name=resolved_name,
            type=resolved_type,
            api_version=api_version,
            location=location,
            properties=resolved_properties,
            depends_on=resolved_depends,
            category=category,
            json_path=json_path,
            raw_json=res,
            tags=tags
        )

    def _extract_child_resources(self, raw_res: Dict[str, Any], parent: NormalizedResource, parent_path: str) -> List[NormalizedResource]:
        """Extract child resources defined either in properties (e.g. subnets) or in nested 'resources' array."""
        children = []
        # Case 1: Nested resources array inside a resource
        nested_resources = raw_res.get("resources", [])
        if isinstance(nested_resources, list):
            for c_idx, c_res in enumerate(nested_resources):
                c_path = f"{parent_path}.resources[{c_idx}]"
                child = self._process_resource(c_res, c_path, parent=parent)
                if child:
                    # Inherit dependency to parent automatically
                    if parent.id not in child.depends_on:
                        child.depends_on.append(parent.id)
                    children.append(child)

        # Case 2: Subnets declared inside virtualNetworks properties
        if parent.type.lower() == "microsoft.network/virtualnetworks":
            subnets = raw_res.get("properties", {}).get("subnets", [])
            if isinstance(subnets, list):
                for s_idx, s_data in enumerate(subnets):
                    if isinstance(s_data, dict):
                        s_name = str(self._resolve_value(s_data.get("name", f"subnet_{s_idx}")))
                        s_props = self._resolve_deep(s_data.get("properties", {}))
                        s_id = f"{parent.id}/subnets/{s_name}"
                        child_res = NormalizedResource(
                            id=s_id,
                            name=f"{parent.name}/{s_name}",
                            type="Microsoft.Network/virtualNetworks/subnets",
                            api_version=parent.api_version,
                            location=parent.location,
                            properties=s_props,
                            depends_on=[parent.id],
                            category="Network",
                            json_path=f"{parent_path}.properties.subnets[{s_idx}]",
                            raw_json=s_data
                        )
                        children.append(child_res)

        return children

    def _resolve_value(self, val: Any) -> Any:
        if isinstance(val, str) and val.startswith("[") and val.endswith("]"):
            evaluated = self.evaluator.evaluate(val)
            if isinstance(evaluated, str) and evaluated.startswith("[Unresolved:"):
                self.warnings.append(f"Could not fully resolve expression: {val}")
            return evaluated
        return val

    def _resolve_deep(self, obj: Any) -> Any:
        """Recursively resolve expressions across dictionaries and lists."""
        if isinstance(obj, dict):
            return {k: self._resolve_deep(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
        elif isinstance(obj, list):
            return [self._resolve_deep(item) for item in obj]
        elif isinstance(obj, str):
            return self._resolve_value(obj)
        return obj

import os
import json
import time
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

class AIResponseCache:
    """
    Deterministic response cache for AI analysis results.
    Prevents duplicate calls, optimizes latency, and protects user API quotas.
    Keyed by SHA-256 hash of (file_id, feature_name, params).
    """

    def __init__(self, cache_dir: Optional[str] = None, ttl_seconds: int = 3600, enabled: bool = True):
        self.enabled = enabled
        self.ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
        self.memory_cache: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        self.cache_dir = Path(cache_dir) if cache_dir else None

        if self.cache_dir:
            try:
                os.makedirs(self.cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
            except Exception:
                pass

    def get(
        self,
        file_id: str,
        feature: str,
        params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Retrieves cached response if available and not expired."""
        if not self.enabled:
            return None

        key = self._generate_key(file_id, feature, params)
        now = time.time()

        # 1. Check in-memory cache
        if key in self.memory_cache:
            entry = self.memory_cache[key]
            if now - entry["timestamp"] <= self.ttl_seconds:
                return entry["data"]
            else:
                del self.memory_cache[key]

        # 2. Check disk cache
        if self.cache_dir:
            file_path = self.cache_dir / f"{key}.json"
            if file_path.exists():
                try:
                    with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                        entry = json.load(f)
                    if now - entry.get("timestamp", 0) <= self.ttl_seconds:
                        self.memory_cache[key] = entry
                        return entry.get("data")
                    else:
                        file_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass

        return None

    def set(
        self,
        file_id: str,
        feature: str,
        data: Dict[str, Any],
        params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    ):
        """Stores response in memory and disk cache."""
        if not self.enabled:
            return

        key = self._generate_key(file_id, feature, params)
        now = time.time()
        entry = {
            "file_id": file_id,
            "feature": feature,
            "timestamp": now,
            "data": data
        }

        self.memory_cache[key] = entry

        if self.cache_dir:
            try:
                file_path = self.cache_dir / f"{key}.json"
                with open(file_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    json.dump(entry, f)
            except Exception:
                pass

    def clear(self, file_id: Optional[str] = None):
        """Clears memory cache and disk cache for a specific file or completely."""
        if file_id:
            keys_to_remove = [k for k, v in self.memory_cache.items() if v.get("file_id") == file_id]
            for k in keys_to_remove:
                del self.memory_cache[k]
        else:
            self.memory_cache.clear()

    def _generate_key(self, file_id: str, feature: str, params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
        param_str = json.dumps(params or {}, sort_keys=True)
        raw = f"{file_id}:{feature}:{param_str}"
        return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
