/**
 * Azure ARM Security Analyzer - AI Threat Intelligence & Attack-Path Controller
 * Manages attack-path synthesis, server-side caching, UI timeline rendering,
 * and topology visualizer path highlighting.
 */

let activeAttackPaths = [];
let selectedPathId = null;

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  setupRightPaneTabs();
  setupThreatModelTriggers();
  checkAiStatus();
});

function setupRightPaneTabs() {
  const btnAudit = document.getElementById('tabAuditFindings');
  const btnAi = document.getElementById('tabAiThreats');
  const viewAudit = document.getElementById('viewAuditFindings');
  const viewAi = document.getElementById('viewAiThreats');

  if (!btnAudit || !btnAi || !viewAudit || !viewAi) return;

  btnAudit.addEventListener('click', () => {
    btnAudit.classList.add('active');
    btnAi.classList.remove('active');
    viewAudit.classList.remove('hidden');
    viewAi.classList.add('hidden');
  });

  btnAi.addEventListener('click', () => {
    btnAi.classList.add('active');
    btnAudit.classList.remove('active');
    viewAi.classList.remove('hidden');
    viewAudit.classList.add('hidden');

    // Auto-load if not loaded yet
    if (activeAttackPaths.length === 0 && !window.aiThreatsLoading) {
      loadAiThreatModel(false);
    }
  });
}

function setupThreatModelTriggers() {
  const btnRun = document.getElementById('btnRunThreatModel');
  if (btnRun) {
    btnRun.addEventListener('click', () => {
      loadAiThreatModel(true);
    });
  }

  const btnClearHighlight = document.getElementById('btnClearAttackPath');
  if (btnClearHighlight) {
    btnClearHighlight.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (typeof window.clearAttackPathHighlight === 'function') {
        window.clearAttackPathHighlight();
      }
      document.querySelectorAll('.attack-path-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('selected-path'));
      btnClearHighlight.classList.add('hidden');
    });
  }
}

function checkAiStatus() {
  // Status indicator removed per user request
}

function loadAiThreatModel(forceRefresh = false) {
  const fileId = window.FILE_ID;
  if (!fileId) return;

  const container = document.getElementById('aiThreatsListContainer');
  const btnRun = document.getElementById('btnRunThreatModel');
  const badge = document.getElementById('aiThreatCountBadge');
  window.aiThreatsLoading = true;

  if (btnRun) {
    btnRun.disabled = true;
    btnRun.innerHTML = `<span>⏳</span> Analyzing Attack Paths...`;
  }

  if (container) {
    container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="ai-loading-state">
        <div class="ai-spinner"></div>
        <p class="ai-loading-title">Synthesizing Infrastructure Threat Model...</p>
        <p class="ai-loading-sub">Correlating open ports, NSG boundaries, identity permissions, and data flow channels.</p>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  const url = `/api/ai/threat-model/${fileId}${forceRefresh ? '?refresh=true' : ''}`;

  fetch(url, { method: 'POST' })
    .then(res => res.json())
    .then(data => {
      window.aiThreatsLoading = false;
      if (btnRun) {
        btnRun.disabled = false;
        btnRun.innerHTML = `<span>🔄</span> Refresh Threat Model`;
      }

      if (!data.success) {
        renderAiError(container, data.error, data.code);
        return;
      }

      activeAttackPaths = data.attack_paths || [];
      if (badge) {
        badge.textContent = activeAttackPaths.length;
        badge.className = activeAttackPaths.length > 0 ? 'badge badge-danger' : 'badge badge-subtle';
      }

      renderAttackPaths(container, activeAttackPaths, data.cached);
    })
    .catch(err => {
      window.aiThreatsLoading = false;
      if (btnRun) {
        btnRun.disabled = false;
        btnRun.innerHTML = `<span>🔄</span> Retry Threat Model`;
      }
      renderAiError(container, "Network error reaching AI analysis endpoint: " + err, "NETWORK_ERROR");
    });
}

function renderAttackPaths(container, paths, isCached) {
  if (!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = '';

  if (paths.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="ai-empty-state">
        <span class="ai-empty-icon">🛡️</span>
        <h3>No Correlated Attack Paths Detected</h3>
        <p>The AI model did not identify a viable lateral movement path from external entry points to critical assets based on current configuration evidence.</p>
      </div>
    `;
    return;
  }

  const headerNotice = document.createElement('div');
  headerNotice.className = 'ai-paths-header-notice';
  headerNotice.innerHTML = `
    <span>⚡ Identified <strong>${paths.length} Potential Attack Path${paths.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}</strong></span>
    ${isCached ? '<span class="badge badge-subtle">Cached Result</span>' : '<span class="badge badge-success">Fresh AI Analysis</span>'}
  `;
  container.appendChild(headerNotice);

  paths.forEach((path, idx) => {
    const card = document.createElement('div');
    const riskClass = (path.risk || 'MEDIUM').toLowerCase();
    card.className = `attack-path-card border-risk-${riskClass}`;
    card.setAttribute('data-path-id', path.id);

    const stepsHtml = (path.steps || []).map(s => `
      <div class="path-step-item">
        <div class="step-marker">${s.step}</div>
        <div class="step-content">
          <div class="step-res-line">
            <span class="step-res-id" title="${escapeHtml(s.resource_id)}">${escapeHtml(shortenResourceId(s.resource_id))}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="step-desc">${escapeHtml(s.description)}</div>
          ${s.evidence && s.evidence.length ? `
            <div class="step-evidence">
              <span>Evidence:</span> ${escapeHtml(s.evidence.join('; '))}
            </div>
          ` : ''}
          ${s.preconditions && s.preconditions.length ? `
            <div class="step-preconditions">
              <span>Preconditions:</span> ${escapeHtml(s.preconditions.join('; '))}
            </div>
          ` : ''}
        </div>
      </div>
    `).join('');

    card.innerHTML = `
      <div class="path-card-header">
        <div class="path-title-row">
          <span class="pill pill-${riskClass}">${path.risk}</span>
          <span class="badge badge-subtle">${path.evidence_strength || 'CONFIGURATION_SUPPORTED'}</span>
          <span class="path-title">${escapeHtml(path.title)}</span>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-cyan btn-highlight-path" data-path-idx="${idx}">
          <span>🎯 Highlight in Graph</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <p class="path-summary">${escapeHtml(path.summary)}</p>

      <div class="path-steps-timeline">
        ${stepsHtml}
      </div>

      <div class="path-footer-meta">
        <div><strong>Impact:</strong> ${escapeHtml(path.impact || 'Unauthorized access')}</div>
        ${path.recommendations && path.recommendations.length ? `
          <div class="path-recommendation"><strong>Mitigation:</strong> ${escapeHtml(path.recommendations[0])}</div>
        ` : ''}
      </div>
    `;

    const highlightBtn = card.querySelector('.btn-highlight-path');
    if (highlightBtn) {
      highlightBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        selectAndHighlightPath(path, card);
      });
    }

    container.appendChild(card);
  });
}

function selectAndHighlightPath(path, cardEl) {
  selectedPathId = path.id;

  document.querySelectorAll('.attack-path-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('selected-path'));
  if (cardEl) cardEl.classList.add('selected-path');

  const btnClear = document.getElementById('btnClearAttackPath');
  if (btnClear) btnClear.classList.remove('hidden');

  if (typeof window.highlightAttackPath === 'function') {
    window.highlightAttackPath(path);
  }
}

function renderAiError(container, message, code) {
  if (!container) return;

  let helpHint = "";
  const isOllama = (message && message.toLowerCase().includes("ollama")) || code === "MODEL_NOT_FOUND" || code === "CONNECTION_FAILED";

  if (isOllama) {
    helpHint = `
      <div class="ai-help-box">
        <strong>Ollama Local Setup:</strong>
        <p>1. Start the daemon: <code>ollama serve</code></p>
        <p>2. Pull your chosen model: <code>ollama pull &lt;model&gt;</code> (e.g. qwen, mistral, llama3.2)</p>
        <p>3. Configure <code>OLLAMA_MODEL=&lt;model&gt;</code> in <code>.env</code></p>
      </div>
    `;
  } else if (code === "AI_UNCONFIGURED" || code === "AUTH_FAILED") {
    helpHint = `
      <div class="ai-help-box">
        <strong>Setup Guide:</strong>
        <p>1. Open <code>.env</code> in your project directory.</p>
        <p>2. Set <code>GEMINI_API_KEY=your_gemini_api_key_here</code> or switch to <code>AI_PROVIDER=ollama</code>.</p>
        <p>3. Restart the server or refresh this view.</p>
      </div>
    `;
  } else if (code === "QUOTA_EXCEEDED") {
    helpHint = `
      <div class="ai-help-box">
        <p>Your Google Gemini API quota has been reached. You can switch to local inference by setting <code>AI_PROVIDER=ollama</code> in <code>.env</code>, or inspect deterministic findings.</p>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="ai-error-banner">
      <span class="ai-error-icon">⚠️</span>
      <div class="ai-error-body">
        <h4>AI Analysis Unavailable</h4>
        <p>${escapeHtml(message)}</p>
        ${helpHint}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

function shortenResourceId(fullId) {
  if (!fullId) return "";
  const parts = fullId.split('/');
  return parts[parts.length - 1];
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  if (!str) return '';
  return String(str)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
}

window.explainFinding = function(findingId, containerEl) {
  if (!containerEl) return;
  const fileId = window.FILE_ID;
  if (!fileId) return;

  containerEl.style.display = 'block';
  containerEl.innerHTML = `
    <div class="ai-loading-state" style="padding: 1rem;">
      <div class="ai-spinner" style="width: 22px; height: 22px;"></div>
      <p class="ai-loading-title" style="font-size: 0.82rem;">Synthesizing Architectural Context...</p>
    </div>
  `;

  fetch(`/api/ai/explain/${fileId}/${encodeURIComponent(findingId)}`, { method: 'POST' })
    .then(res => res.json())
    .then(data => {
      if (!data.success) {
        containerEl.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#f87171;">⚠️ ${escapeHtml(data.error || 'Failed generating explanation.')}</span>`;
        return;
      }
      const exp = data.explanation || {};
      const stepsHtml = (exp.verification_steps || []).map(s => `<li>${escapeHtml(s)}</li>`).join('');

      containerEl.innerHTML = `
        <div class="ai-insight-panel">
          <div class="ai-insight-title">
            <span>⚡ AI Architectural Analysis</span>
            ${exp.cached ? '<span class="badge badge-subtle">Cached</span>' : '<span class="badge badge-success">Fresh</span>'}
          </div>
          <div class="ai-insight-section">
            <strong>Risk Summary:</strong>
            <p>${escapeHtml(exp.summary)}</p>
          </div>
          <div class="ai-insight-section">
            <strong>Architecture & Blast Radius:</strong>
            <p>${escapeHtml(exp.architectural_context)}</p>
          </div>
          <div class="ai-insight-section">
            <strong>Attacker Perspective:</strong>
            <p>${escapeHtml(exp.attacker_perspective)}</p>
          </div>
          ${stepsHtml ? `
            <div class="ai-insight-section">
              <strong>Verification Steps:</strong>
              <ol style="padding-left: 1.15rem; margin-top: 0.2rem;">${stepsHtml}</ol>
            </div>
          ` : ''}
          <div class="ai-insight-section" style="border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06); padding-top: 0.35rem;">
            <strong style="color: #34d399;">Remediation Recommendation:</strong>
            <p>${escapeHtml(exp.quick_fix_summary)}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    })
    .catch(err => {
      containerEl.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#f87171;">⚠️ Network error: ${escapeHtml(err.message || String(err))}</span>`;
    });
};

window.remediateFinding = function(findingId, containerEl) {
  if (!containerEl) return;
  const fileId = window.FILE_ID;
  if (!fileId) return;

  containerEl.style.display = 'block';
  containerEl.innerHTML = `
    <div class="ai-loading-state" style="padding: 1rem;">
      <div class="ai-spinner" style="width: 22px; height: 22px;"></div>
      <p class="ai-loading-title" style="font-size: 0.82rem;">Generating ARM JSON Patch...</p>
    </div>
  `;

  fetch(`/api/ai/remediate/${fileId}/${encodeURIComponent(findingId)}`, { method: 'POST' })
    .then(res => res.json())
    .then(data => {
      if (!data.success) {
        containerEl.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#f87171;">⚠️ ${escapeHtml(data.error || 'Failed generating remediation patch.')}</span>`;
        return;
      }
      const rem = data.remediation || {};
      const patchProps = rem.suggested_properties_patch || {};

      containerEl.innerHTML = `
        <div class="ai-insight-panel">
          <div class="ai-insight-title">
            <span>🛠️ AI Remediation Patch</span>
            ${rem.cached ? '<span class="badge badge-subtle">Cached</span>' : '<span class="badge badge-success">Fresh</span>'}
          </div>
          <div class="ai-insight-section">
            <p>${escapeHtml(rem.explanation)}</p>
          </div>
          <div class="ai-patch-box">
            <pre><code>${escapeHtml(rem.code_snippet || JSON.stringify(patchProps, null, 2))}</code></pre>
          </div>
          <div style="display: flex; gap: 0.5rem; align-items: center; margin-top: 0.35rem; flex-wrap: wrap;">
            <button class="btn btn-xs btn-outline-green btn-run-rescan">
              <span>🔬 Dry-Run Rescan Verification</span>
            </button>
            <span class="rescan-result-slot"></span>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;

      const btnRescan = containerEl.querySelector('.btn-run-rescan');
      const rescanSlot = containerEl.querySelector('.rescan-result-slot');
      if (btnRescan) {
        btnRescan.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          btnRescan.disabled = true;
          btnRescan.innerHTML = `<span>⏳ Rescanning...</span>`;
          fetch(`/api/ai/remediate/${fileId}/${encodeURIComponent(findingId)}/rescan`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ patch_properties: patchProps })
          })
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(vData => {
              btnRescan.disabled = false;
              btnRescan.innerHTML = `<span>🔬 Dry-Run Rescan Verification</span>`;
              if (vData.success && vData.verification) {
                const v = vData.verification;
                if (v.resolved) {
                  rescanSlot.innerHTML = `<span class="ai-dryrun-badge badge-verified-fixed">✅ Verified: Target finding resolved!</span>`;
                } else {
                  rescanSlot.innerHTML = `<span class="ai-dryrun-badge badge-still-vulnerable">⚠️ Still vulnerable (${v.remaining_rule_violations} violation remaining)</span>`;
                }
              } else {
                rescanSlot.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#f87171; font-size:0.75rem;">Rescan check error: ${escapeHtml(vData.error || 'Unknown')}</span>`;
              }
            })
            .catch(err => {
              btnRescan.disabled = false;
              btnRescan.innerHTML = `<span>🔬 Dry-Run Rescan Verification</span>`;
              rescanSlot.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#f87171; font-size:0.75rem;">Rescan failed: ${escapeHtml(err.message)}</span>`;
            });
        });
      }
    })
    .catch(err => {
      containerEl.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#f87171;">⚠️ Network error: ${escapeHtml(err.message || String(err))}</span>`;
    });
};



================================================================================
FILE PATH: app\static\js\dashboard.js
================================================================================
/**
 * Main dashboard coordinator module.
 */
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  // Safely extract bootstrapped data
  let initialData = window.INITIAL_DATA || {};
  const rawDataEl = document.getElementById('initialData');
  if (rawDataEl && rawDataEl.textContent) {
    try {
      initialData = JSON.parse(rawDataEl.textContent);
    } catch(e) {
      console.error("Error parsing embedded initialData:", e);
    }
  }

  // 1. Initialize Findings Panel First (Guaranteed execution)
  try {
    if (initialData.findings && typeof initFindings === 'function') {
      initFindings(initialData.findings);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Error initializing findings panel:", err);
  }

  // 2. Initialize Topology Graph (Cytoscape or Native SVG)
  try {
    if (initialData.graph && typeof initCytoscapeGraph === 'function') {
      initCytoscapeGraph(initialData.graph);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Error initializing topology graph:", err);
  }

  // 3. Toolbar actions (Works for both Cytoscape and SVG)
  const btnZoomIn = document.getElementById('btnZoomIn');
  const btnZoomOut = document.getElementById('btnZoomOut');
  const btnFit = document.getElementById('btnFit');
  const selectLayout = document.getElementById('selectLayout');

  if (btnZoomIn) {
    btnZoomIn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (window.cy) {
        cy.zoom(cy.zoom() * 1.25);
      } else {
        const panGroup = document.getElementById('svgPanGroup');
        if (panGroup && window.svgState) {
          svgState.zoom *= 1.2;
          panGroup.setAttribute('transform', `translate(${svgState.panX}, ${svgState.panY}) scale(${svgState.zoom})`);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  if (btnZoomOut) {
    btnZoomOut.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (window.cy) {
        cy.zoom(cy.zoom() * 0.8);
      } else {
        const panGroup = document.getElementById('svgPanGroup');
        if (panGroup && window.svgState) {
          svgState.zoom *= 0.8;
          panGroup.setAttribute('transform', `translate(${svgState.panX}, ${svgState.panY}) scale(${svgState.zoom})`);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  if (btnFit) {
    btnFit.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (window.cy) {
        cy.fit(null, 40);
        cy.center();
      } else {
        const panGroup = document.getElementById('svgPanGroup');
        if (panGroup && window.svgState) {
          svgState.zoom = 1;
          svgState.panX = 0;
          svgState.panY = 0;
          panGroup.setAttribute('transform', `translate(0, 0) scale(1)`);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  const btnToggleExpand = document.getElementById('btnToggleExpand');
  if (btnToggleExpand) {
    btnToggleExpand.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const workspace = document.querySelector('.workspace-grid');
      const findingsPane = document.querySelector('.findings-pane');
      if (workspace) {
        const isNowExpanded = workspace.classList.toggle('graph-fullscreen');
        if (findingsPane) {
          findingsPane.classList.toggle('pane-hidden');
        }
        btnToggleExpand.classList.toggle('active', isNowExpanded);
        btnToggleExpand.title = isNowExpanded ? "Restore Split View (Show Findings)" : "Expand Visualizer (Hide Findings)";
        btnToggleExpand.innerHTML = isNowExpanded ? `
          <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
            <path d="M4 14h6v6M20 10h-6V4M14 10l7-7M3 21l7-7"></path>
          </svg>
        ` : `
          <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
            <path d="M15 3h6v6M9 21H3v-6M21 3l-7 7M3 21l7-7"></path>
          </svg>
        `;
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (window.cy) {
            cy.resize();
            cy.fit(null, 40);
          }
        }, 150);
      }
    });
  }

  const btnToggleTraffic = document.getElementById('btnToggleTraffic');
  if (btnToggleTraffic) {
    btnToggleTraffic.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (typeof window.toggleLiveTraffic === 'function') {
        window.toggleLiveTraffic();
      }
    });
  }

  if (selectLayout) {
    selectLayout.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      if (typeof applyLayout === 'function') {
        applyLayout(e.target.value);
      }
    });
  }

  // 4. Node search filter
  const searchInput = document.getElementById('nodeSearchInput');
  if (searchInput) {
    searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      const query = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (window.cy) {
        if (!query) {
          cy.elements().removeClass('dimmed highlighted');
          return;
        }
        cy.nodes().forEach(node => {
          const label = (node.data('label') || '').toLowerCase();
          const type = (node.data('type') || '').toLowerCase();
          if (label.includes(query) || type.includes(query)) {
            node.removeClass('dimmed').addClass('highlighted');
          } else {
            node.removeClass('highlighted').addClass('dimmed');
          }
        });
      } else {
        // SVG Search highlight
        document.querySelectorAll('.svg-node').forEach(nodeEl => {
          const text = nodeEl.textContent.toLowerCase();
          nodeEl.style.opacity = (!query || text.includes(query)) ? '1' : '0.2';
        });
      }
    });
  }

  // 5. Risk filter pills
  const riskPills = document.querySelectorAll('#riskFilterGroup .filter-pill');
  riskPills.forEach(pill => {
    pill.addEventListener('click', () => {
      riskPills.forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
      pill.classList.add('active');
      const selectedRisk = pill.getAttribute('data-risk');

      if (window.cy) {
        if (selectedRisk === 'ALL') {
          cy.elements().removeClass('dimmed highlighted');
        } else {
          cy.nodes().forEach(node => {
            const risk = (node.data('risk_level') || 'CLEAN').toUpperCase();
            if (risk === selectedRisk) {
              node.removeClass('dimmed').addClass('highlighted');
            } else {
              node.removeClass('highlighted').addClass('dimmed');
            }
          });
        }
      } else {
        // SVG Risk filter
        document.querySelectorAll('.svg-node').forEach(nodeEl => {
          const nId = nodeEl.getAttribute('data-node-id');
          const nodeData = (initialData.graph.nodes || []).find(n => n.data.id === nId);
          if (!nodeData) return;
          const risk = (nodeData.data.risk_level || 'CLEAN').toUpperCase();
          nodeEl.style.opacity = (selectedRisk === 'ALL' || risk === selectedRisk) ? '1' : '0.2';
        });
      }
    });
  });

  // 6. Close Inspector Drawer
  const btnCloseInspector = document.getElementById('btnCloseInspector');
  if (btnCloseInspector) {
    btnCloseInspector.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (typeof closeResourceInspector === 'function') {
        closeResourceInspector();
      }
      if (typeof clearHighlights === 'function') {
        clearHighlights();
      }
    });
  }
});


================================================================================

/**
 * Findings module for managing, filtering, and interacting with security audit findings.
 */
let allFindings = [];
let currentSeverityFilter = 'ALL';
let currentResourceFilter = null;

function initFindings(findingsData) {
  allFindings = findingsData || [];
  renderFindings();
  setupFindingsTabs();
}

function setupFindingsTabs() {
  const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#findingsTabs .tab-btn');
  tabs.forEach(tab => {
    tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
      tabs.forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
      tab.classList.add('active');
      currentSeverityFilter = tab.getAttribute('data-tab');
      renderFindings();
    });
  });
}

function renderFindings() {
  const container = document.getElementById('findingsListContainer');
  if (!container) return;

  container.innerHTML = '';

  const filtered = allFindings.filter(f => {
    const matchesSev = (currentSeverityFilter === 'ALL' || f.severity.toUpperCase() === currentSeverityFilter);
    const matchesRes = (!currentResourceFilter || f.affected_resource_id.toLowerCase() === currentResourceFilter.toLowerCase());
    return matchesSev && matchesRes;
  });

  const countBadge = document.getElementById('findingsCountBadge');
  if (countBadge) {
    countBadge.textContent = filtered.length;
  }

  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = `
      <div style="text-align: center; padding: 2rem 1rem; color: #8b949e; font-size: 0.9rem;">
        <span>✅ No findings match the active criteria.</span>
      </div>
    `;
    return;
  }

  filtered.forEach(finding => {
    const card = document.createElement('div');
    const sevClass = getSeverityClass(finding.severity);
    card.className = `finding-card ${sevClass}`;
    card.setAttribute('data-finding-id', finding.finding_id);

    card.innerHTML = `
      <div class="finding-top">
        <div class="finding-badges">
          <span class="badge ${finding.severity.toUpperCase() === 'CRITICAL' ? 'badge-danger' : 'badge-subtle'}">${finding.severity}</span>
          <span class="badge badge-subtle">${finding.category}</span>
        </div>
        <span class="finding-rule-id">${finding.rule_id}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="finding-title">${escapeHtml(finding.title)}</div>
      <div class="finding-resource">
        <span>📍 Affected: <strong>${escapeHtml(finding.affected_resource_name)}</strong></span>
      </div>
      <div class="finding-details-collapsible">
        <div><strong>Evidence:</strong></div>
        <div class="evidence-box">${escapeHtml(finding.evidence)}</div>
        <div style="margin-top: 0.25rem;"><strong>Impact:</strong> ${escapeHtml(finding.impact)}</div>
        <div style="margin-top: 0.25rem;"><strong>Remediation:</strong></div>
        <div class="remediation-box">${escapeHtml(finding.remediation)}</div>
        <div style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: #8b949e; margin-top: 0.25rem;">
          <code>${escapeHtml(finding.json_path)}</code>
        </div>
        <div class="finding-ai-actions">
          <button class="btn btn-xs btn-outline-cyan btn-ai-explain" data-fid="${finding.finding_id}">
            <span>⚡ AI Explain</span>
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-xs btn-outline-green btn-ai-fix" data-fid="${finding.finding_id}">
            <span>🛠️ AI Fix</span>
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="finding-ai-container" id="aiBox_${finding.finding_id}" style="display: none;"></div>
      </div>
    `;

    const btnExplain = card.querySelector('.btn-ai-explain');
    const btnFix = card.querySelector('.btn-ai-fix');
    const aiBox = card.querySelector(`#aiBox_${finding.finding_id}`);

    if (btnExplain && aiBox) {
      btnExplain.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (typeof window.explainFinding === 'function') {
          window.explainFinding(finding.finding_id, aiBox);
        }
      });
    }

    if (btnFix && aiBox) {
      btnFix.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (typeof window.remediateFinding === 'function') {
          window.remediateFinding(finding.finding_id, aiBox);
        }
      });
    }

    card.addEventListener('click', () => {
      document.querySelectorAll('.finding-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('selected'));
      card.classList.add('selected');
      if (window.focusNodeById) {
        window.focusNodeById(finding.affected_resource_id);
      }
    });

    container.appendChild(card);
  });
}

function getSeverityClass(sev) {
  switch ((sev || '').toUpperCase()) {
    case 'CRITICAL': return 'crit';
    case 'HIGH': return 'high';
    case 'MEDIUM': return 'med';
    case 'LOW': return 'low';
    default: return 'low';
  }
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  if (!str) return '';
  return String(str)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
}

window.filterFindingsByResource = function(resourceId) {
  currentResourceFilter = resourceId;
  renderFindings();
};

window.resetFindingsFilter = function() {
  currentResourceFilter = null;
  renderFindings();
};

};

function getAzureIconKey(type, category) {
  const lowType = (type || '').toLowerCase();
  const lowCat = (category || '').toLowerCase();

  if (lowType.includes('virtualmachine') && !lowType.includes('scaleset')) return 'vm';
  if (lowType.includes('virtualnetwork') && !lowType.includes('subnet')) return 'vnet';
  if (lowType.includes('subnet')) return 'subnet';
  if (lowType.includes('networksecuritygroup')) return 'nsg';
  if (lowType.includes('networkinterface')) return 'nic';
  if (lowType.includes('publicipaddress')) return 'pip';
  if (lowType.includes('storageaccount')) return 'storage';
  if (lowType.includes('keyvault')) return 'keyvault';
  if (lowType.includes('sites') && !lowType.includes('serverfarm')) return 'webapp';
  if (lowType.includes('serverfarm')) return 'plan';
  if (lowType.includes('sql') || lowType.includes('databases')) return 'sql';
  if (lowType.includes('loadbalancer')) return 'lb';
  if (lowType.includes('applicationgateway')) return 'appgateway';
  if (lowType.includes('userassignedidentities') || lowType.includes('managedidentity')) return 'identity';
  if (lowType.includes('roleassignment') || lowType.includes('roledefinition')) return 'role';
  if (lowType.includes('redis')) return 'redis';
  if (lowType.includes('automation')) return 'automation';

  if (lowCat.includes('compute')) return 'vm';
  if (lowCat.includes('storage')) return 'storage';
  if (lowCat.includes('app service')) return 'webapp';
  if (lowCat.includes('database')) return 'sql';
  if (lowCat.includes('security')) return 'nsg';

  return 'generic';
}

function getAzureIconSvg(type, category) {
  const key = getAzureIconKey(type, category);
  return AZURE_SVG_ICONS[key] || AZURE_SVG_ICONS.generic;
}

function getIconDataUri(type, category) {
  const svg = getAzureIconSvg(type, category);
  return 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent(svg);
}

/**
 * Intelligent Cloud Architecture Layout Algorithm:
 * - Groups resources by cloud architectural tier:
 *   Tier 0: Network Ingress & Connectivity (Public IPs, VNets, Subnets, Gateways, NICs, NSGs)
 *   Tier 1: Compute & Workloads (Web Apps, Virtual Machines, App Service Plans, Containers)
 *   Tier 2: Backend Data & Storage (Storage Accounts, SQL Databases, Redis Caches)
 *   Tier 3: Security, Governance & Identity (Key Vaults, Roles, RBAC, Automation)
 * - Renders connected DAGs top-to-bottom and wraps resources in balanced 3-4 column tiers
 * - Guarantees the graph never stretches into an uncontrollable single horizontal line.
 */
function computeArchitecturalLayout(nodes, edges, containerWidth, containerHeight) {
  if (!nodes || nodes.length === 0) return {};

  const width = Math.max(820, containerWidth || 850);
  const height = Math.max(540, containerHeight || 550);
  const nodeW = 230;
  const nodeH = 76;
  const gapX = 40;
  const gapY = 55;

  const positions = {};

  const inDegree = {};
  const adj = {};
  const nodeMap = {};

  nodes.forEach(n => {
    const id = n.data.id;
    inDegree[id] = 0;
    adj[id] = [];
    nodeMap[id] = n;
  });

  (edges || []).forEach(e => {
    const s = e.data.source;
    const t = e.data.target;
    if (inDegree[t] !== undefined && inDegree[s] !== undefined) {
      inDegree[t]++;
      adj[s].push(t);
    }
  });

  function getTier(node) {
    const type = (node.data.type || '').toLowerCase();
    const cat = (node.data.category || '').toLowerCase();
    if (type.includes('network') || type.includes('virtualnetwork') || type.includes('subnet') || type.includes('publicip') || type.includes('loadbalancer') || type.includes('applicationgateway')) {
      return 0; // Network Tier
    }
    if (type.includes('virtualmachine') || type.includes('sites') || type.includes('serverfarm') || type.includes('managedclusters')) {
      return 1; // Compute & Workloads Tier
    }
    if (type.includes('storage') || type.includes('sql') || type.includes('databases') || type.includes('redis') || cat.includes('database')) {
      return 2; // Data & Storage Tier
    }
    return 3; // Security, Identity & Governance Tier
  }

  const tiers = { 0: [], 1: [], 2: [], 3: [] };
  nodes.forEach(n => {
    tiers[getTier(n)].push(n);
  });

  // Balanced tier-based cloud architecture layout
  let currentY = 40;
  const tierKeys = [0, 1, 2, 3];

  tierKeys.forEach(tKey => {
    const tierNodes = tiers[tKey];
    if (tierNodes.length === 0) return;

    // Wrap tier nodes in rows of 3 to 4 nodes
    const maxCols = Math.min(4, Math.max(2, tierNodes.length));
    const numSubRows = Math.ceil(tierNodes.length / maxCols);

    for (let r = 0; r < numSubRows; r++) {
      const slice = tierNodes.slice(r * maxCols, (r + 1) * maxCols);
      const cols = slice.length;
      const totalRowW = cols * nodeW + (cols - 1) * gapX;
      const startX = Math.max(40, (width - totalRowW) / 2);

      slice.forEach((node, colIdx) => {
        positions[node.data.id] = {
          x: startX + colIdx * (nodeW + gapX),
          y: currentY + r * (nodeH + gapY)
        };
      });
    }

    currentY += numSubRows * (nodeH + gapY);
  });

  return positions;
}

/**
 * Main initialization entry point for the topology graph.
 */
function initCytoscapeGraph(graphData) {
  const container = document.getElementById('cy');
  if (!container || !graphData || !graphData.nodes) return;
  currentGraphData = graphData;

  // Validate and sanitize edges to guarantee that both source & target exist in nodes
  const nodeIds = new Set(graphData.nodes.map(n => n.data.id));
  const validEdges = (graphData.edges || []).filter(e => {
    return e && e.data && nodeIds.has(e.data.source) && nodeIds.has(e.data.target);
  });

  const elements = [
    ...graphData.nodes.map(n => ({ group: 'nodes', data: n.data })),
    ...validEdges.map(e => ({ group: 'edges', data: e.data }))
  ];

  // Try Cytoscape if available on window
  if (typeof cytoscape !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      cy = cytoscape({
        container: container,
        elements: elements,
        boxSelectionEnabled: false,
        autounselectify: false,
        wheelSensitivity: 0.2,
        style: [
          // Professional Azure Resource Card Node
          {
            selector: 'node',
            style: {
              'shape': 'round-rectangle',
              'width': 230,
              'height': 76,
              'z-index': 10, // Nodes always sit on top of edges
              'background-color': '#131b2e',
              'border-width': 2.5,
              'border-color': function(ele) {
                const risk = ele.data('risk_level') || 'CLEAN';
                return RISK_COLORS[risk] || '#475569';
              },
              'border-opacity': 1,
              // Vector SVG Icon on the left
              'background-image': function(ele) {
                return getIconDataUri(ele.data('type'), ele.data('category'));
              },
              'background-width': '38px',
              'background-height': '38px',
              'background-position-x': '16px',
              'background-position-y': '50%',
              'background-clip': 'none',
              'background-fit': 'none',
              // Clear typography inside the card
              'label': function(ele) {
                const name = truncate(ele.data('label') || ele.data('id'), 18);
                const shortType = ele.data('type_short') || ele.data('category') || 'Resource';
                const count = ele.data('finding_count') || 0;
                const badge = count > 0 ? `\n[ ${count} Issue${count > 1 ? 's' : ''} ]` : '';
                return `${name}\n${shortType}${badge}`;
              },
              'color': '#f8fafc',
              'font-size': 11,
              'font-weight': '700',
              'font-family': '-apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif',
              'text-valign': 'center',
              'text-halign': 'center',
              'text-margin-x': 24, // Shift text right to leave clearance for 40px icon
              'text-wrap': 'wrap',
              'text-max-width': 145,
              'line-height': 1.35,
              'transition-property': 'border-width, border-color, underlay-opacity',
              'transition-duration': '0.2s'
            }
          },
          {
            selector: 'node:selected',
            style: {
              'border-width': 4.5,
              'border-color': '#38bdf8',
              'underlay-color': '#38bdf8',
              'underlay-padding': 6,
              'underlay-opacity': 0.45,
              'z-index': 20
            }
          },
          {
            selector: 'node.highlighted',
            style: {
              'border-width': 4.5,
              'border-color': '#38bdf8',
              'underlay-color': '#38bdf8',
              'underlay-padding': 6,
              'underlay-opacity': 0.45,
              'z-index': 20
            }
          },
          {
            selector: 'node.path-entry',
            style: {
              'border-width': 5.5,
              'border-color': '#f97316',
              'underlay-color': '#f97316',
              'underlay-padding': 10,
              'underlay-opacity': 0.55,
              'z-index': 30
            }
          },
          {
            selector: 'node.path-target',
            style: {
              'border-width': 5.5,
              'border-color': '#ef4444',
              'underlay-color': '#ef4444',
              'underlay-padding': 10,
              'underlay-opacity': 0.55,
              'z-index': 30
            }
          },
          {
            selector: 'node.dimmed',
            style: {
              'opacity': 0.2
            }
          },
          // Base Edge Styling - Live Animated Electric Wire drawn behind nodes
          {
            selector: 'edge',
            style: {
              'z-index': 1,
              'width': 2.5,
              'curve-style': 'bezier',
              'target-arrow-shape': 'triangle',
              'target-arrow-color': '#64748b',
              'line-color': '#64748b',
              'line-style': 'dashed',
              'line-dash-pattern': [8, 5],
              'line-dash-offset': 0,
              'arrow-scale': 1.25,
              'opacity': 0.85,
              'label': '', // Clean! Never permanently label every edge
              'text-background-color': '#090d16',
              'text-background-opacity': 0.95,
              'text-background-padding': 3,
              'text-background-shape': 'round-rectangle'
            }
          },
          // On-Demand Relationship Labeling: Appears on edge hover, selection, or node click
          {
            selector: 'edge.hovered, edge:selected, edge.highlighted',
            style: {
              'label': 'data(label)',
              'font-size': 10.5,
              'font-weight': '600',
              'color': '#f8fafc',
              'text-rotation': 'autorotate',
              'z-index': 25,
              'width': 3.8,
              'opacity': 1
            }
          },
          // Relationship-specific color tokens (matching legend)
          // 1. External Exposure Wire (Public IP -> Ingress)
          {
            selector: 'edge[relation_type = "exposure"]',
            style: {
              'line-color': '#f97316',
              'target-arrow-color': '#f97316',
              'line-dash-pattern': [9, 5]
            }
          },
          // 2. Network Association / Internal Traffic Wire
          {
            selector: 'edge[relation_type = "network_association"]',
            style: {
              'line-color': '#38bdf8',
              'target-arrow-color': '#38bdf8',
              'line-dash-pattern': [8, 5]
            }
          },
          // 3. Application Data Flow Wire (App -> Storage, Redis, DB)
          {
            selector: 'edge[relation_type = "data_flow"]',
            style: {
              'line-color': '#10b981',
              'target-arrow-color': '#10b981',
              'line-dash-pattern': [7, 4]
            }
          },
          // 4. Secret & Security Integration Wire (App -> Key Vault)
          {
            selector: 'edge[relation_type = "integration"]',
            style: {
              'line-color': '#c084fc',
              'target-arrow-color': '#c084fc',
              'line-dash-pattern': [8, 5]
            }
          },
          // 5. Network Security Boundary Wire (NSG -> NIC/Subnet)
          {
            selector: 'edge[relation_type = "security_boundary"]',
            style: {
              'line-color': '#a855f7',
              'target-arrow-color': '#a855f7',
              'line-dash-pattern': [6, 4]
            }
          },
          // 6. Role / Authorization Wire
          {
            selector: 'edge[relation_type = "authorization"]',
            style: {
              'line-color': '#eab308',
              'target-arrow-color': '#eab308',
              'line-dash-pattern': [4, 4]
            }
          },
          // 7. General Architecture Dependency
          {
            selector: 'edge[relation_type = "dependency"]',
            style: {
              'line-color': '#94a3b8',
              'target-arrow-color': '#94a3b8',
              'line-dash-pattern': [7, 5]
            }
          },
          // 8. Hosting & Containment
          {
            selector: 'edge[relation_type = "hosting"]',
            style: {
              'line-color': '#06b6d4',
              'target-arrow-color': '#06b6d4'
            }
          },
          {
            selector: 'edge[relation_type = "containment"]',
            style: {
              'line-color': '#94a3b8',
              'target-arrow-color': '#94a3b8'
            }
          },
          {
            selector: 'edge[relation_type = "monitoring"]',
            style: {
              'line-color': '#10b981',
              'target-arrow-color': '#10b981'
            }
          },
          {
            selector: 'edge.dimmed',
            style: {
              'opacity': 0.12
            }
          }
        ]
      });

      // Expose globally
      window.cy = cy;

      // Apply initial intelligent architectural layout
      applyLayout('dagre');

      // Start live wire traffic flow animation
      startCytoscapeTrafficAnimation();

      // Edge hover interaction: show relationship detail on hover
      cy.on('mouseover', 'edge', function(evt) {
        evt.target.addClass('hovered');
      });
      cy.on('mouseout', 'edge', function(evt) {
        evt.target.removeClass('hovered');
      });

      // Node selection listeners
      cy.on('select', 'node', function(evt) {
        const node = evt.target;
        inspectResource(node.data());
        highlightNodeAndNeighborhood(node);
        if (window.filterFindingsByResource) {
          window.filterFindingsByResource(node.id());
        }
      });

      cy.on('tap', function(evt) {
        if (evt.target === cy) {
          clearHighlights();
          closeResourceInspector();
          if (window.resetFindingsFilter) {
            window.resetFindingsFilter();
          }
        }
      });

      // Window resize listener
      window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
        if (cy) {
          cy.resize();
        }
      });

      return;
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("Cytoscape initialization issue, falling back to Native SVG Visualizer:", err);
    }
  }

  // Pure SVG Interactive Visualizer fallback
  renderSvgFallbackGraph(graphData, validEdges, currentLayoutName);
}

/**
 * High-performance smooth traffic animation loop for Cytoscape.js
 * Moves dash offsets to simulate live incoming packets and outgoing data requests.
 */
function startCytoscapeTrafficAnimation() {
  if (trafficAnimFrame) {
    cancelAnimationFrame(trafficAnimFrame);
    trafficAnimFrame = null;
  }

  function step() {
    if (isTrafficFlowing && cy) {
      trafficOffset = (trafficOffset - 0.72) % 26;
      cy.batch(() => {
        // Incoming external traffic marches into the infrastructure
        cy.edges('[traffic_flow = "incoming"]').style('line-dash-offset', -trafficOffset);
        // Outgoing data flows to Storage, Cache, DB, Secrets march outward
        cy.edges('[traffic_flow = "outgoing"]').style('line-dash-offset', trafficOffset);
        // Forward network associations & dependencies march forward
        cy.edges('[traffic_flow = "forward"]').style('line-dash-offset', trafficOffset);
        // Policy / boundary wires pulse gently
        cy.edges('[traffic_flow = "control"]').style('line-dash-offset', trafficOffset * 0.5);
      });
    }
    trafficAnimFrame = requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }

  trafficAnimFrame = requestAnimationFrame(step);
}

/**
 * Toggles live traffic flow animation.
 */
function toggleLiveTraffic() {
  isTrafficFlowing = !isTrafficFlowing;
  const btn = document.getElementById('btnToggleTraffic');
  if (btn) {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', isTrafficFlowing);
    btn.classList.toggle('btn-traffic-paused', !isTrafficFlowing);
    btn.title = isTrafficFlowing ? "Live Wire Traffic: Active (Click to Pause)" : "Live Wire Traffic: Paused (Click to Resume)";
  }

  const container = document.getElementById('cy');
  if (container) {
    container.classList.toggle('traffic-paused', !isTrafficFlowing);
  }
}

/**
 * Layout-switching function with Intelligent Architectural Layout as the default Dagre engine.
 */
function applyLayout(layoutName) {
  if (!layoutName) layoutName = 'dagre';
  currentLayoutName = layoutName;

  const selectLayout = document.getElementById('selectLayout');
  if (selectLayout && selectLayout.value !== layoutName) {
    selectLayout.value = layoutName;
  }

  if (cy) {
    const container = document.getElementById('cy');
    const width = container ? container.clientWidth : 850;
    const height = container ? container.clientHeight : 550;

    if (layoutName === 'dagre') {
      const calculatedPositions = computeArchitecturalLayout(
        currentGraphData.nodes,
        currentGraphData.edges,
        width,
        height
      );

      cy.batch(() => {
        cy.nodes().forEach(node => {
          const pos = calculatedPositions[node.id()];
          if (pos) {
            node.position(pos);
          }
        });
      });

      cy.fit(null, 40);
      cy.center();
      return;
    } else if (layoutName === 'cose') {
      cy.layout({
        name: 'cose',
        animate: true,
        animationDuration: 500,
        refresh: 20,
        fit: true,
        padding: 40,
        randomize: false,
        componentSpacing: 80,
        nodeRepulsion: function() { return 4500; },
        nodeOverlap: 40,
        idealEdgeLength: function() { return 110; },
        edgeElasticity: function() { return 64; },
        gravity: 50
      }).run();
    } else if (layoutName === 'breadthfirst') {
      cy.layout({
        name: 'breadthfirst',
        directed: true,
        spacingFactor: 1.4,
        padding: 40,
        animate: true,
        animationDuration: 400,
        fit: true
      }).run();
    } else if (layoutName === 'grid') {
      cy.layout({
        name: 'grid',
        fit: true,
        padding: 40,
        animate: true,
        animationDuration: 400,
        avoidOverlap: true,
        spacingFactor: 1.2
      }).run();
    }
  } else if (currentGraphData) {
    renderSvgFallbackGraph(currentGraphData, null, layoutName);
  }
}

function highlightNodeAndNeighborhood(node) {
  if (!cy) return;
  cy.elements().removeClass('highlighted dimmed hovered');
  const neighborhood = node.neighborhood().add(node);
  cy.elements().not(neighborhood).addClass('dimmed');
  neighborhood.addClass('highlighted');
}

function clearHighlights() {
  if (cy) {
    cy.elements().removeClass('highlighted dimmed hovered path-entry path-target');
  } else {
    document.querySelectorAll('.svg-node').forEach(n => {
      n.classList.remove('selected');
      const r = n.querySelector('rect');
      if (r) r.setAttribute('stroke-width', '2.5');
    });
  }
}

window.highlightAttackPath = function(pathData) {
  if (!pathData || !pathData.steps || pathData.steps.length === 0) return;

  const pathResourceIds = new Set(pathData.steps.map(s => (s.resource_id || '').toLowerCase()));

  if (cy) {
    cy.batch(() => {
      cy.elements().addClass('dimmed').removeClass('highlighted path-entry path-target');

      // Highlight participating nodes
      const pathNodes = cy.nodes().filter(node => pathResourceIds.has(node.id().toLowerCase()));
      pathNodes.removeClass('dimmed').addClass('highlighted');

      // Highlight Entry Point (step 1)
      const firstStepId = (pathData.steps[0].resource_id || '').toLowerCase();
      const entryNode = cy.nodes().filter(n => n.id().toLowerCase() === firstStepId);
      if (entryNode.length > 0) {
        entryNode.addClass('path-entry');
      }

      // Highlight Objective/Target (last step)
      if (pathData.steps.length > 1) {
        const lastStepId = (pathData.steps[pathData.steps.length - 1].resource_id || '').toLowerCase();
        const targetNode = cy.nodes().filter(n => n.id().toLowerCase() === lastStepId);
        if (targetNode.length > 0) {
          targetNode.addClass('path-target');
        }
      }

      // Highlight existing connecting edges between participating path nodes
      const pathEdges = cy.edges().filter(edge => {
        return pathResourceIds.has(edge.source().id().toLowerCase()) &&
               pathResourceIds.has(edge.target().id().toLowerCase());
      });
      pathEdges.removeClass('dimmed').addClass('highlighted');
    });

    const activeElements = cy.elements('.highlighted');
    if (activeElements.length > 0) {
      cy.animate({
        fit: { eles: activeElements, padding: 60 }
      }, { duration: 450 });
    }
  }
};

window.clearAttackPathHighlight = function() {
  clearHighlights();
  if (cy) {
    cy.fit(null, 40);
  }
};

function focusNodeById(resourceId) {
  if (!resourceId) return;

  if (cy) {
    const node = cy.getElementById(resourceId);
    if (node && node.length > 0) {
      highlightNodeAndNeighborhood(node);
      cy.animate({ center: { eles: node }, zoom: 1.15 }, { duration: 400 });
      inspectResource(node.data());
    }
  } else {
    const svgNode = document.querySelector(`[data-node-id="${resourceId}"]`);
    if (svgNode) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.svg-node').forEach(n => {
        n.classList.remove('selected');
        const r = n.querySelector('rect');
        if (r) r.setAttribute('stroke-width', '2.5');
      });
      svgNode.classList.add('selected');
      const rect = svgNode.querySelector('rect');
      if (rect) rect.setAttribute('stroke-width', '5');

      if (currentGraphData && currentGraphData.nodes) {
        const nodeData = currentGraphData.nodes.find(n => n.data.id === resourceId);
        if (nodeData) inspectResource(nodeData.data);
      }
    }
  }
}

function inspectResource(nodeData) {
  const drawer = document.getElementById('resourceInspector');
  const body = document.getElementById('inspectorBody');
  const nameLabel = document.getElementById('inspectResourceName');
  const riskBadge = document.getElementById('inspectResourceRisk');
  const typeLabel = document.getElementById('inspectResourceType');
  const locLabel = document.getElementById('inspectResourceLocation');
  const apiLabel = document.getElementById('inspectResourceApiVersion');
  const pathLabel = document.getElementById('inspectResourcePath');
  const jsonCode = document.getElementById('inspectResourcePropertiesJson');

  if (!drawer || !body) return;

  const risk = nodeData.risk_level || 'CLEAN';
  const riskColor = RISK_COLORS[risk] || '#475569';
  const shortType = nodeData.type_short || nodeData.category || 'Azure Resource';

  nameLabel.innerHTML = `<strong>${escapeHtml(nodeData.label || nodeData.id)}</strong> <span style="font-size: 0.82rem; color: #94a3b8; margin-left: 0.5rem;">(${escapeHtml(shortType)})</span>`;
  riskBadge.textContent = risk;
  riskBadge.style.backgroundColor = riskColor;
  riskBadge.style.color = '#ffffff';

  typeLabel.textContent = nodeData.type || 'N/A';
  locLabel.textContent = nodeData.location || 'Global / Resource Group';
  apiLabel.textContent = nodeData.api_version || 'N/A';
  pathLabel.textContent = nodeData.json_path || 'N/A';

  // Associated findings section
  let findingsContainer = document.getElementById('inspectResourceFindingsList');
  if (!findingsContainer) {
    const findingsDiv = document.createElement('div');
    findingsDiv.id = 'inspectResourceFindingsSection';
    findingsDiv.innerHTML = `
      <div style="margin-top: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.35rem;">
        <strong>Associated Security Findings:</strong>
        <span class="badge" id="inspectResourceFindingsCount" style="margin-left: 0.4rem;"></span>
      </div>
      <div id="inspectResourceFindingsList" style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.4rem; max-height: 140px; overflow-y: auto;"></div>
    `;
    body.insertBefore(findingsDiv, body.querySelector('.inspector-json-section'));
    findingsContainer = document.getElementById('inspectResourceFindingsList');
  }

  const findingsCountEl = document.getElementById('inspectResourceFindingsCount');
  const findingsList = nodeData.findings || [];
  if (findingsCountEl) {
    findingsCountEl.textContent = `${findingsList.length} Finding${findingsList.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;
    findingsCountEl.className = `badge ${findingsList.length > 0 ? 'badge-danger' : 'badge-clean'}`;
  }

  if (findingsContainer) {
    if (findingsList.length === 0) {
      findingsContainer.innerHTML = `<span style="font-size: 0.82rem; color: #10b981;">✅ No security misconfigurations identified on this resource.</span>`;
    } else {
      findingsContainer.innerHTML = findingsList.map(f => `
        <div class="inspector-finding-pill" data-finding-id="${escapeHtml(f.finding_id)}" style="background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.12); border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3); padding: 0.35rem 0.6rem; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 0.82rem; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; cursor: pointer;">
          <span><strong>${escapeHtml(f.rule_id)}</strong>: ${escapeHtml(f.title)}</span>
          <span class="badge" style="background:${RISK_COLORS[f.severity] || '#ef4444'}; color:#fff; font-size: 0.68rem;">${escapeHtml(f.severity)}</span>
        </div>
      `).join('');

      findingsContainer.querySelectorAll('.inspector-finding-pill').forEach(pill => {
        pill.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const fId = pill.getAttribute('data-finding-id');
          const findingCard = document.querySelector(`.finding-card[data-finding-id="${fId}"]`);
          if (findingCard) {
            findingCard.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
            findingCard.click();
          }
        });
      });
    }
  }

  // Associated relationships section
  let relsContainer = document.getElementById('inspectResourceRelsList');
  if (!relsContainer) {
    const relsDiv = document.createElement('div');
    relsDiv.id = 'inspectResourceRelsSection';
    relsDiv.innerHTML = `
      <div style="margin-top: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.35rem;">
        <strong>Topology Relationships:</strong>
      </div>
      <div id="inspectResourceRelsList" style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 0.4rem; font-size: 0.8rem;"></div>
    `;
    body.insertBefore(relsDiv, body.querySelector('.inspector-json-section'));
    relsContainer = document.getElementById('inspectResourceRelsList');
  }

  if (relsContainer && currentGraphData && currentGraphData.edges) {
    const rId = nodeData.id;
    const connectedEdges = currentGraphData.edges.filter(e => e.data.source === rId || e.data.target === rId);
    if (connectedEdges.length === 0) {
      relsContainer.innerHTML = `<span style="color: #94a3b8;">Isolated resource (no direct dependencies or network links detected).</span>`;
    } else {
      relsContainer.innerHTML = connectedEdges.map(e => {
        const isSource = e.data.source === rId;
        const otherId = isSource ? e.data.target : e.data.source;
        const otherNode = currentGraphData.nodes.find(n => n.data.id === otherId);
        const otherName = otherNode ? (otherNode.data.label || otherId) : otherId;
        const arrow = isSource ? '➔' : '⬅';
        return `
          <span class="inspector-rel-tag" data-target-id="${escapeHtml(otherId)}" style="background: #1c263b; border: 1px solid #243147; padding: 0.25rem 0.55rem; border-radius: 4px; color: #f8fafc; cursor: pointer;">
            ${arrow} <em>${escapeHtml(e.data.label || e.data.relation_type)}</em>: <strong>${escapeHtml(truncate(otherName, 18))}</strong>
          </span>
        `;
      }).join('');

      relsContainer.querySelectorAll('.inspector-rel-tag').forEach(tag => {
        tag.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const tId = tag.getAttribute('data-target-id');
          focusNodeById(tId);
        });
      });
    }
  }

  jsonCode.textContent = JSON.stringify(nodeData.properties || {}, null, 2);
  body.classList.remove('hidden');
}

function closeResourceInspector() {
  const body = document.getElementById('inspectorBody');
  const nameLabel = document.getElementById('inspectResourceName');
  if (body) body.classList.add('hidden');
  if (nameLabel) nameLabel.textContent = "Select a node in the graph to inspect configuration";
}

/**
 * Built-in Native SVG Interactive Topology Renderer (100% Offline)
 * - Renders crisp professional Azure resource cards.
 * - Draws live animated wires with flowing pulses and packet particles.
 * - Supports native pan, zoom, and fit interactions.
 */
function renderSvgFallbackGraph(graphData, validEdges, layoutName) {
  const container = document.getElementById('cy');
  if (!container || !graphData || !graphData.nodes) return;

  const currentLayout = layoutName || currentLayoutName || 'dagre';
  const nodes = graphData.nodes;
  const edges = validEdges || graphData.edges || [];

  const width = Math.max(820, container.clientWidth || 850);
  const height = Math.max(540, container.clientHeight || 550);
  const nodeW = 230;
  const nodeH = 76;

  let positions = {};
  if (currentLayout === 'grid') {
    const cols = Math.min(4, Math.max(2, Math.ceil(Math.sqrt(nodes.length * 1.3))));
    const gapX = 40;
    const gapY = 55;
    const numRows = Math.ceil(nodes.length / cols);
    const startY = Math.max(40, (height - (numRows * nodeH + (numRows - 1) * gapY)) / 2);
    nodes.forEach((node, idx) => {
      const col = idx % cols;
      const row = Math.floor(idx / cols);
      const rowCols = Math.min(cols, nodes.length - row * cols);
      const rowWidth = rowCols * nodeW + (rowCols - 1) * gapX;
      const startX = Math.max(40, (width - rowWidth) / 2);
      positions[node.data.id] = {
        x: startX + col * (nodeW + gapX),
        y: startY + row * (nodeH + gapY)
      };
    });
  } else if (currentLayout === 'cose') {
    const centerX = width / 2;
    const centerY = height / 2;
    const radius = Math.min(width * 0.38, Math.max(180, nodes.length * 28));
    nodes.forEach((node, idx) => {
      const angle = (2 * Math.PI * idx) / nodes.length;
      positions[node.data.id] = {
        x: centerX + radius * Math.cos(angle) - nodeW / 2,
        y: centerY + (radius * 0.72) * Math.sin(angle) - nodeH / 2
      };
    });
  } else {
    positions = computeArchitecturalLayout(nodes, edges, width, height);
  }

  let svgHtml = `
    <svg id="svgTopologyCanvas" width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}" style="background:#080c14; cursor: grab;">
      <defs>
        <marker id="svgArrowCyan" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="8" refY="5" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" orient="auto-start-reverse">
          <path d="M 0 1 L 10 5 L 0 9 z" fill="#38bdf8" />
        </marker>
        <marker id="svgArrowOrange" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="8" refY="5" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" orient="auto-start-reverse">
          <path d="M 0 1 L 10 5 L 0 9 z" fill="#f97316" />
        </marker>
        <marker id="svgArrowGreen" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="8" refY="5" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" orient="auto-start-reverse">
          <path d="M 0 1 L 10 5 L 0 9 z" fill="#10b981" />
        </marker>
        <marker id="svgArrowPurple" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="8" refY="5" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" orient="auto-start-reverse">
          <path d="M 0 1 L 10 5 L 0 9 z" fill="#c084fc" />
        </marker>
        <marker id="svgArrowYellow" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="8" refY="5" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" orient="auto-start-reverse">
          <path d="M 0 1 L 10 5 L 0 9 z" fill="#eab308" />
        </marker>
        <marker id="svgArrowSlate" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="8" refY="5" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" orient="auto-start-reverse">
          <path d="M 0 1 L 10 5 L 0 9 z" fill="#64748b" />
        </marker>
      </defs>
      <g id="svgPanGroup" transform="translate(${svgState.panX}, ${svgState.panY}) scale(${svgState.zoom})">
        <!-- Live Animated Wires Group: Drawn FIRST behind nodes -->
        <g id="svgEdgesGroup">
  `;

  // Draw Edges behind nodes with animated dashed wires and traveling packet particles
  edges.forEach(edge => {
    const p1 = positions[edge.data.source];
    const p2 = positions[edge.data.target];
    if (p1 && p2) {
      const x1 = p1.x + nodeW / 2;
      const y1 = p1.y + nodeH / 2;
      const x2 = p2.x + nodeW / 2;
      const y2 = p2.y + nodeH / 2;

      let color = '#64748b';
      let marker = 'url(#svgArrowSlate)';
      let trafficClass = 'live-traffic-forward';
      let animDur = 2.4;

      const rel = edge.data.relation_type;
      const flow = edge.data.traffic_flow || 'forward';

      if (rel === 'network_association') {
        color = '#38bdf8';
        marker = 'url(#svgArrowCyan)';
        trafficClass = 'live-traffic-forward';
        animDur = 2.2;
      } else if (rel === 'exposure') {
        color = '#f97316';
        marker = 'url(#svgArrowOrange)';
        trafficClass = 'live-traffic-incoming';
        animDur = 1.8;
      } else if (rel === 'data_flow') {
        color = '#10b981';
        marker = 'url(#svgArrowGreen)';
        trafficClass = 'live-traffic-forward';
        animDur = 2.0;
      } else if (rel === 'integration') {
        color = '#c084fc';
        marker = 'url(#svgArrowPurple)';
        trafficClass = 'live-traffic-forward';
        animDur = 2.4;
      } else if (rel === 'security_boundary') {
        color = '#a855f7';
        marker = 'url(#svgArrowPurple)';
        trafficClass = 'live-traffic-pulse';
        animDur = 3.0;
      } else if (rel === 'authorization') {
        color = '#eab308';
        marker = 'url(#svgArrowYellow)';
        trafficClass = 'live-traffic-forward';
        animDur = 2.5;
      } else if (rel === 'hosting') {
        color = '#06b6d4';
        marker = 'url(#svgArrowCyan)';
        trafficClass = 'live-traffic-forward';
        animDur = 2.4;
      } else if (rel === 'dependency') {
        color = '#64748b';
        marker = 'url(#svgArrowSlate)';
        trafficClass = 'live-traffic-forward';
        animDur = 2.8;
      }

      svgHtml += `
        <g class="svg-edge-wire-group" data-edge-id="${edge.data.id}">
          <!-- Wire subtle glow base -->
          <line x1="${x1}" y1="${y1}" x2="${x2}" y2="${y2}" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="4.5" opacity="0.18" />
          <!-- Live traffic wire -->
          <line class="svg-edge-line ${trafficClass}" data-edge-id="${edge.data.id}" x1="${x1}" y1="${y1}" x2="${x2}" y2="${y2}" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="2.3" stroke-dasharray="8 5" marker-end="${marker}" opacity="0.85">
            <title>${escapeSvg(edge.data.label || rel)}</title>
          </line>
          <!-- Traveling traffic packet particle -->
          <circle r="3.2" fill="${color}" opacity="0.95">
            <animateMotion dur="${animDur}s" repeatCount="indefinite" path="M ${x1} ${y1} L ${x2} ${y2}" />
          </circle>
        </g>
      `;
    }
  });

  svgHtml += `</g><!-- Nodes Group: Drawn on top of edges -->
    <g id="svgNodesGroup">`;

  // Draw Nodes on top of edges
  nodes.forEach(node => {
    const p = positions[node.data.id] || { x: 50, y: 50 };
    const risk = node.data.risk_level || 'CLEAN';
    const borderColor = RISK_COLORS[risk] || '#475569';
    const label = node.data.label || node.data.id;
    const shortType = node.data.type_short || node.data.category || 'Azure Resource';
    const iconSvg = getAzureIconSvg(node.data.type, node.data.category);

    svgHtml += `
      <g class="svg-node" data-node-id="${node.data.id}" transform="translate(${p.x}, ${p.y})" style="cursor: pointer;">
        <!-- Card Background -->
        <rect width="${nodeW}" height="${nodeH}" rx="10" fill="#131b2e" stroke="${borderColor}" stroke-width="2.5" />
        <!-- Left Azure Service Icon -->
        <g transform="translate(14, 18)">
          ${iconSvg}
        </g>
        <!-- Resource Name -->
        <text x="64" y="32" fill="#f8fafc" font-size="12.5" font-weight="700">${escapeSvg(truncate(label, 17))}</text>
        <!-- Resource Type -->
        <text x="64" y="50" fill="#94a3b8" font-size="10.5" font-weight="500">${escapeSvg(shortType)}</text>
        <!-- Status Indicator Pill -->
        ${node.data.finding_count > 0 ? `
          <rect x="176" y="8" width="46" height="20" rx="10" fill="${borderColor}" />
          <text x="199" y="22" fill="#ffffff" font-size="10" font-weight="700" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ ${node.data.finding_count}</text>
        ` : `
          <circle cx="210" cy="18" r="5" fill="#10b981" />
        `}
        <!-- Complete Name Tooltip -->
        <title>${escapeSvg(label)} (${escapeSvg(node.data.type)})</title>
      </g>
    `;
  });

  svgHtml += `</g></g></svg>`;
  container.innerHTML = svgHtml;

  // Bind interactive SVG events
  const svgCanvas = document.getElementById('svgTopologyCanvas');
  const panGroup = document.getElementById('svgPanGroup');

  if (svgCanvas && panGroup) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.svg-node').forEach(nodeEl => {
      nodeEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        const nId = nodeEl.getAttribute('data-node-id');
        document.querySelectorAll('.svg-node rect').forEach(r => r.setAttribute('stroke-width', '2.5'));
        nodeEl.querySelector('rect').setAttribute('stroke-width', '5');

        const nodeData = nodes.find(n => n.data.id === nId);
        if (nodeData) {
          inspectResource(nodeData.data);
          if (window.filterFindingsByResource) {
            window.filterFindingsByResource(nId);
          }
        }
      });
    });

    svgCanvas.addEventListener('click', () => {
      document.querySelectorAll('.svg-node rect').forEach(r => r.setAttribute('stroke-width', '2.5'));
      closeResourceInspector();
      if (window.resetFindingsFilter) {
        window.resetFindingsFilter();
      }
    });

    svgCanvas.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const delta = e.deltaY > 0 ? 0.9 : 1.1;
      svgState.zoom = Math.max(0.4, Math.min(3, svgState.zoom * delta));
      panGroup.setAttribute('transform', `translate(${svgState.panX}, ${svgState.panY}) scale(${svgState.zoom})`);
    }, { passive: false });

    svgCanvas.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
      if (e.target.closest('.svg-node')) return;
      svgState.isDragging = true;
      svgState.startX = e.clientX - svgState.panX;
      svgState.startY = e.clientY - svgState.panY;
      svgCanvas.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
    });

    window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
      if (!svgState.isDragging) return;
      svgState.panX = e.clientX - svgState.startX;
      svgState.panY = e.clientY - svgState.startY;
      panGroup.setAttribute('transform', `translate(${svgState.panX}, ${svgState.panY}) scale(${svgState.zoom})`);
    });

    window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
      if (svgState.isDragging) {
        svgState.isDragging = false;
        if (svgCanvas) svgCanvas.style.cursor = 'grab';
      }
    });
  }
}

function truncate(str, max) {
  if (!str) return '';
  return str.length > max ? str.substring(0, max - 2) + '..' : str;
}

function escapeSvg(str) {
  if (!str) return '';
  return String(str).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  if (!str) return '';
  return String(str)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
}

// Window globals
window.applyLayout = applyLayout;
window.focusNodeById = focusNodeById;
window.clearHighlights = clearHighlights;
window.inspectResource = inspectResource;
window.closeResourceInspector = closeResourceInspector;
window.toggleLiveTraffic = toggleLiveTraffic;

        <span class="legend-item"><span class="legend-edge edge-sec"></span> NSG Boundary</span>
        <span class="legend-item"><span class="legend-edge edge-auth"></span> Role / Auth</span>
        <span class="legend-item"><span class="legend-edge edge-dep"></span> Dependency</span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Right Pane: Dual-Mode Workspace (Audit Findings & AI Attack Paths) -->
    <div class="card findings-pane">
      <div class="pane-header">
        <div class="pane-tabs-group" id="rightPaneTabs">
          <button class="pane-tab-btn active" id="tabAuditFindings" data-pane="audit">
            <span>🛡️ Findings</span>

<script>
  const dropZone = document.getElementById('dropZone');
  const fileInput = document.getElementById('template_file');
  const selectedChip = document.getElementById('selectedFileChip');
  const nameLabel = document.getElementById('selectedFileName');
  const sizeLabel = document.getElementById('selectedFileSize');
  const removeBtn = document.getElementById('btnRemoveFile');

  // Drag and drop visual cues
  ['dragenter', 'dragover'].forEach(eventName => {
    dropZone.addEventListener(eventName, (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      dropZone.classList.add('drag-active');
    }, false);
  });

  ['dragleave', 'drop'].forEach(eventName => {
    dropZone.addEventListener(eventName, (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      dropZone.classList.remove('drag-active');
    }, false);
  });

  dropZone.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
    const files = e.dataTransfer.files;
    if (files.length) {
      fileInput.files = files;
      showSelectedFile(files[0]);
    }
  });

  fileInput.addEventListener('change', () => {
    if (fileInput.files.length) {
      showSelectedFile(fileInput.files[0]);
    }
  });

  if (removeBtn) {
    removeBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      fileInput.value = '';
      selectedChip.classList.add('hidden');
    });
  }

  function showSelectedFile(file) {
    nameLabel.textContent = file.name;
    sizeLabel.textContent = `(${(file.size / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB)`;
    selectedChip.classList.remove('hidden');
  }

  function loadSample(sampleName) {
    const btn = event.target.closest('button');
    const originalText = btn.innerHTML;
    btn.innerHTML = "<span>⏳</span> Loading...";
    btn.disabled = true;

    fetch(`/api/load-sample/${sampleName}`, { method: 'POST' })
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(data => {
        if (data.success && data.redirect_url) {
          window.location.href = data.redirect_url;
        } else {
          alert("Error loading sample: " + (data.error || "Unknown error"));
          btn.innerHTML = originalText;
          btn.disabled = false;
        }
      })
      .catch(err => {
        alert("Network error loading sample: " + err);
        btn.innerHTML = originalText;
        btn.disabled = false;
      });
  }
</script>
{% endblock %}

FILE PATH: app\utils\sanitization.py
================================================================================
import os
import uuid
import re
from pathlib import Path

SENSITIVE_KEY_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r'password', re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r'secret', re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r'connectionstring', re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r'privatekey', re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r'certkey', re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r'apikey', re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r'adminpassword', re.IGNORECASE),
]

def generate_secure_storage_id() -> str:
    """Generate a collision-free UUID4-based internal identifier."""
    return str(uuid.uuid4())

def get_storage_path(upload_dir: str, file_id: str) -> Path:
    """
    Get full storage path for a file ID in the upload directory.
    Guards strictly against directory traversal by verifying resolved parent path.
    """
    if not file_id or not isinstance(file_id, str):
        raise ValueError("Invalid file ID provided.")

    if re.search(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]', file_id) or ".." in file_id or "/" in file_id or "\\" in file_id:
        raise ValueError("Invalid file ID causing directory traversal attempt.")

    base = Path(upload_dir).resolve()
    target = (base / f"{file_id}.json").resolve()
    
    if not str(target).startswith(str(base)):
        raise ValueError("Invalid file ID causing directory traversal attempt.")
    
    return target

def mask_sensitive_data(data):
    """
    Recursively traverse dictionary/list structures and mask known secret values.
    Returns a sanitized copy.
    """
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        sanitized = {}
        for key, value in data.items():
            if any(pattern.search(str(key)) for pattern in SENSITIVE_KEY_PATTERNS):
                sanitized[key] = "********"
            else:
                sanitized[key] = mask_sensitive_data(value)
        return sanitized
    elif isinstance(data, list):
        return [mask_sensitive_data(item) for item in data]
    return data

FILE PATH: app\utils\validation.py
================================================================================
import json
from typing import Tuple, Dict, Any, List

KNOWN_ARM_SCHEMAS = [
    "schema.management.azure.com/schemas/2014-04-01-preview/deploymenttemplate.json",
    "schema.management.azure.com/schemas/2015-01-01/deploymenttemplate.json",
    "schema.management.azure.com/schemas/2018-05-01/subscriptiondeploymenttemplate.json",
    "schema.management.azure.com/schemas/2019-04-01/deploymenttemplate.json",
    "schema.management.azure.com/schemas/2019-08-01/tenantdeploymenttemplate.json",
    "schema.management.azure.com/schemas/2019-08-01/managementgroupdeploymenttemplate.json",
]

def allowed_file(filename: str, allowed_extensions: set) -> bool:
    """Check if the uploaded file has a permitted extension."""
    return '.' in filename and filename.rsplit('.', 1)[1].lower() in allowed_extensions

def validate_arm_content(content_str: str) -> Tuple[bool, str, Dict[str, Any], List[str]]:
    """
    Validates ARM template content string.
    Returns:
        (is_valid: bool, status_code: str, parsed_data: dict, warnings_or_errors: list[str])
    
    Status codes:
        - 'VALID': Well-formed ARM template
        - 'VALID_WITH_WARNINGS': Valid ARM template but contains caveats or missing optional sections
        - 'INVALID_JSON': Malformed JSON syntax
        - 'STRUCTURALLY_INVALID': Valid JSON but not an Azure ARM template
    """
    # 1. Syntax check
    try:
        data = json.loads(content_str)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        return False, "INVALID_JSON", {}, [f"Invalid JSON syntax at line {e.lineno}, column {e.colno}: {e.msg}"]

    # 2. Top-level object structure check
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return False, "STRUCTURALLY_INVALID", {}, ["ARM template root must be a JSON object, received " + type(data).__name__]

    warnings = []
    
    # 3. Schema check
    schema = data.get("$schema", "")
    has_arm_schema = False
    if isinstance(schema, str) and "schema.management.azure.com" in schema.lower():
        has_arm_schema = True
    elif schema:
        warnings.append(f"Unrecognized or custom $schema specified: '{schema}'. Expected Azure deployment template schema.")

    # 4. Resources check
    resources = data.get("resources")
    if resources is None:
        if not has_arm_schema:
            return False, "STRUCTURALLY_INVALID", {}, ["JSON does not contain an Azure '$schema' or a 'resources' array."]
        warnings.append("Template contains an Azure $schema but specifies no 'resources' array.")
        data["resources"] = []
    elif not isinstance(resources, list):
        return False, "STRUCTURALLY_INVALID", {}, ["'resources' field must be an array of resource definitions."]

    # 5. Check individual resource structure basics
    for idx, res in enumerate(data.get("resources", [])):
        if not isinstance(res, dict):
            return False, "STRUCTURALLY_INVALID", {}, [f"Resource at index {idx} must be a JSON object."]
        if "type" not in res:
            warnings.append(f"Resource at index {idx} is missing a 'type' property.")
        if "name" not in res:
            warnings.append(f"Resource at index {idx} is missing a 'name' property.")

    # 6. Check contentVersion
    if "contentVersion" not in data:
        warnings.append("Optional 'contentVersion' field was not specified.")

    status = "VALID_WITH_WARNINGS" if warnings else "VALID"
    return True, status, data, warnings
